function applyDarkMode()
	local L=game:GetService("Lighting"); local sky=L:FindFirstChild("slaxerDarkSky") or Instance.new("Sky")
	sky.Name="slaxerDarkSky"; sky.SkyboxBk="rbxassetid://159454299"; sky.SkyboxDn="rbxassetid://159454296"; sky.SkyboxFt="rbxassetid://159454293"; sky.SkyboxLf="rbxassetid://159454286"; sky.SkyboxRt="rbxassetid://159454289"; sky.SkyboxUp="rbxassetid://159454291"; sky.Parent=L
	L.Brightness=0; L.ClockTime=0; L.ExposureCompensation=-2; L.OutdoorAmbient=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
end
function removeDarkMode()
	local L=game:GetService("Lighting"); local s=L:FindFirstChild("slaxerDarkSky"); if s then s:Destroy() end
	L.Brightness=_defBrightness; L.ClockTime=_defClockTime; L.ExposureCompensation=_defExposure; L.OutdoorAmbient=_defOutdoorAmb
end
local accConns={}
function removeAccsFromChar(char) if not char then return end; for _,obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do if obj:IsA("Accessory") or obj:IsA("Hat") then pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end end end
function startRemoveAccs()
	for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do removeAccsFromChar(p.Character) end
	table.insert(accConns,Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p) table.insert(accConns,p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char) task.wait(0.5); if State.removeAccEnabled then removeAccsFromChar(char) end end)) end))
	for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do table.insert(accConns,p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char) task.wait(0.5); if State.removeAccEnabled then removeAccsFromChar(char) end end)) end
end
function stopRemoveAccs() for _,c in ipairs(accConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end; accConns={} end

local _hardHitRing=nil
function showHardHitRing()
	local char=LP.Character; if not char then return end; local hrp2=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if not hrp2 then return end
	if _hardHitRing and _hardHitRing.Parent then return end
	local cyl=Instance.new("CylinderHandleAdornment"); cyl.Name="SLAXERHardHitRing"; cyl.Adornee=hrp2; cyl.Color3=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20); cyl.AlwaysOnTop=true; cyl.Transparency=0
	cyl.Radius=State.hardHitRadius; cyl.InnerRadius=State.hardHitRadius-0.3; cyl.Height=0.15; cyl.CFrame=CFrame.new(0,-3,0); cyl.Parent=hrp2; _hardHitRing=cyl
end
function hideHardHitRing() if _hardHitRing then pcall(function() _hardHitRing:Destroy() end); _hardHitRing=nil end end
local _hardHitConn=nil
function startHardHit()
	if _hardHitConn then return end
	_hardHitConn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not State.hardHitEnabled then return end; local char=LP.Character; if not char then return end; local root=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if not root then return end
		if not _hardHitRing or not _hardHitRing.Parent then showHardHitRing() end
		if _hardHitRing then _hardHitRing.Radius=State.hardHitRadius; _hardHitRing.InnerRadius=State.hardHitRadius-0.3 end
	end)
end
function stopHardHit() if _hardHitConn then _hardHitConn:Disconnect(); _hardHitConn=nil end; hideHardHitRing() end

local _antiLagDescConn=nil
function applyAntiLag(ultra)
	local L=game:GetService("Lighting"); L.GlobalShadows=false; L.FogEnd=1e10; L.Brightness=1; L.EnvironmentDiffuseScale=0; L.EnvironmentSpecularScale=0
	for _,e in pairs(L:GetChildren()) do pcall(function() if e:IsA("BlurEffect") or e:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or e:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or e:IsA("BloomEffect") or e:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then e.Enabled=false end end) end
	for _,obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do pcall(function()
		if obj:IsA("BasePart") then obj.Material=Enum.Material.Plastic; obj.Reflectance=0; if ultra then obj.CastShadow=false end
		elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then if ultra then obj:Destroy() else obj.Transparency=1 end
		elseif ultra and (obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Trail") or obj:IsA("Beam") or obj:IsA("Fire")) then obj.Enabled=false end
	end) end
	if ultra then pcall(function() if setfpscap then setfpscap(999999999) end end) end
end
function enableAntiLag()
	State.antiLagEnabled=true; applyAntiLag(false)
	if _antiLagDescConn then _antiLagDescConn:Disconnect() end
	_antiLagDescConn=workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
		if not State.antiLagEnabled then return end
		pcall(function() if obj:IsA("BasePart") then obj.Material=Enum.Material.Plastic; obj.Reflectance=0 elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then obj.Transparency=1 end end)
	end)
end
function disableAntiLag() State.antiLagEnabled=false; if _antiLagDescConn then _antiLagDescConn:Disconnect(); _antiLagDescConn=nil end end
function enableUltraMode() State.ultraModeEnabled=true; applyAntiLag(true) end
function disableUltraMode() State.ultraModeEnabled=false end

local _espObjects={}; local _espReady=false; local _espESPEnabled=false; local _espTracerEnabled=false
function createESPForPlayer(player)
	local data={}; local lines={}
	for i=1,4 do local l=Drawing.new("Line"); l.Color=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20); l.Thickness=1.5; l.Transparency=1; l.Visible=false; lines[i]=l end
	local healthBG=Drawing.new("Line"); healthBG.Color=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0); healthBG.Thickness=4; healthBG.Transparency=1; healthBG.Visible=false
	local healthFG=Drawing.new("Line"); healthFG.Color=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20); healthFG.Thickness=2.5; healthFG.Transparency=1; healthFG.Visible=false
	local distText=Drawing.new("Text"); distText.Color=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20); distText.Size=11; distText.Center=false; distText.Outline=true; distText.Font=Drawing.Fonts.Plex; distText.Visible=false
	local tracer=Drawing.new("Line"); tracer.Color=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20); tracer.Thickness=1.5; tracer.Transparency=1; tracer.Visible=false
	data.Lines=lines; data.HealthBG=healthBG; data.HealthFG=healthFG; data.DistText=distText; data.TracerLine=tracer; data.Player=player; return data
end
function removeESPPlayer(player)
	if not _espObjects[player] then return end; local d=_espObjects[player]; if type(d)~="table" then _espObjects[player]=nil; return end
	for _,l in pairs(d.Lines) do pcall(function() l:Remove() end) end
	pcall(function() d.HealthBG:Remove() end); pcall(function() d.HealthFG:Remove() end); pcall(function() d.DistText:Remove() end); pcall(function() d.TracerLine:Remove() end); _espObjects[player]=nil
end
function addESPPlayer(player)
	if player==LP then return end
	if _espReady then if not _espObjects[player] or type(_espObjects[player])~="table" then _espObjects[player]=createESPForPlayer(player) end else _espObjects[player]=true end
end
function initESP()
	local timeout=tick()+10; repeat task.wait(0.1) until pcall(function() local t=Drawing.new("Line"); t:Remove() end) or tick()>timeout
	if tick()>timeout then return end; _espReady=true
	for player,val in pairs(_espObjects) do if player~=LP and (val==true or type(val)~="table") then _espObjects[player]=createESPForPlayer(player) end end
	RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if not(_espESPEnabled or _espTracerEnabled) then
			for _,data in pairs(_espObjects) do if type(data)=="table" then for _,l in pairs(data.Lines) do l.Visible=false end; data.HealthBG.Visible=false; data.HealthFG.Visible=false; data.DistText.Visible=false; data.TracerLine.Visible=false end end; return
		end
		local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera; if not cam then return end; local vp=cam.ViewportSize
		for player,data in pairs(_espObjects) do
			if type(data)=="table" then
			local char=player.Character; local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); local head2=char and char:FindFirstChild("Head"); local humanoid=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); local alive=root and humanoid and humanoid.Health>0
			if alive then
				local _,onScreen=cam:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position)
				local function getBBox() if not head2 then return nil end; local topW=Vector3.new(root.Position.X,head2.Position.Y+0.5,root.Position.Z); local botW=Vector3.new(root.Position.X,root.Position.Y-3,root.Position.Z); local ts,ton=cam:WorldToViewportPoint(topW); local bs,bon=cam:WorldToViewportPoint(botW); if not ton and not bon then return nil end; local height=math.abs(ts.Y-bs.Y); local width=height*0.5; local cx=ts.X; return {tl=Vector2.new(cx-width/2,ts.Y),tr=Vector2.new(cx+width/2,ts.Y),bl=Vector2.new(cx-width/2,bs.Y),br=Vector2.new(cx+width/2,bs.Y),cx=cx,top=ts.Y,bottom=bs.Y,left=cx-width/2,right=cx+width/2,height=height} end
				local box=getBBox()
				if _espESPEnabled and box and onScreen then
					local L=data.Lines; local col=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20)
					L[1].From=box.tl; L[1].To=box.tr; L[1].Visible=true; L[1].Color=col
					L[2].From=box.bl; L[2].To=box.br; L[2].Visible=true; L[2].Color=col
					L[3].From=box.tl; L[3].To=box.bl; L[3].Visible=true; L[3].Color=col
					L[4].From=box.tr; L[4].To=box.br; L[4].Visible=true; L[4].Color=col
					local hpPct=math.clamp(humanoid.Health/humanoid.MaxHealth,0,1); local barX=box.left-6
					data.HealthBG.From=Vector2.new(barX,box.top); data.HealthBG.To=Vector2.new(barX,box.bottom); data.HealthBG.Visible=true
					local filledY=box.bottom-(box.height*hpPct); data.HealthFG.From=Vector2.new(barX,filledY); data.HealthFG.To=Vector2.new(barX,box.bottom); data.HealthFG.Color=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20); data.HealthFG.Visible=true
					local dist2=math.floor((cam.CFrame.Position-root.Position).Magnitude); data.DistText.Position=Vector2.new(box.right+5,box.top+(box.height/2)-6); data.DistText.Text=dist2.."m"; data.DistText.Color=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20); data.DistText.Visible=true
				else for _,l in pairs(data.Lines) do l.Visible=false end; data.HealthBG.Visible=false; data.HealthFG.Visible=false; data.DistText.Visible=false end
				if _espTracerEnabled and onScreen then local sp2,_=cam:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position); data.TracerLine.From=Vector2.new(vp.X/2,vp.Y); data.TracerLine.To=Vector2.new(sp2.X,sp2.Y); data.TracerLine.Color=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20); data.TracerLine.Visible=true
				else data.TracerLine.Visible=false end
			else
				for _,l in pairs(data.Lines or {}) do l.Visible=false end
				if data.HealthBG then data.HealthBG.Visible=false end; if data.HealthFG then data.HealthFG.Visible=false end
				if data.DistText then data.DistText.Visible=false end; if data.TracerLine then data.TracerLine.Visible=false end
			end
			end
		end
	end)
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(addESPPlayer); Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(removeESPPlayer)
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p) p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(0.5); addESPPlayer(p) end) end)
for _,p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(0.5); addESPPlayer(p) end) end

local _noCamConn=nil; local _noCamParts={}
function enableNoCamCollision()
	State.noCamCollisionEnabled=true
	if _noCamConn then _noCamConn:Disconnect() end
	_noCamConn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if not State.noCamCollisionEnabled then return end
		local ch=LP.Character; if not ch then return end; local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera; if not cam then return end
		local hrp2=ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if not hrp2 then return end
		local camPos=cam.CFrame.Position; local charPos=hrp2.Position+Vector3.new(0,1.5,0)
		local toChar=charPos-camPos; local dist=toChar.Magnitude; if dist<0.3 then return end
		local params=RaycastParams.new(); params.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude; params.FilterDescendantsInstances={ch}; params.IgnoreWater=true
		local hit={}; local origin=camPos; local remaining=toChar
		for _=1,12 do
			if remaining.Magnitude<0.2 then break end
			local res=workspace:Raycast(origin,remaining,params); if not res then break end
			local p=res.Instance
			if p and p:IsA("BasePart") and not p:IsDescendantOf(ch) then
				hit[p]=true; if _noCamParts[p]==nil then _noCamParts[p]=p.LocalTransparencyModifier end; p.LocalTransparencyModifier=1
			end
			origin=res.Position+remaining.Unit*0.02; remaining=charPos-origin
		end
		for p,orig in pairs(_noCamParts) do if not hit[p] then pcall(function() if p and p.Parent then p.LocalTransparencyModifier=orig end end); _noCamParts[p]=nil end end
	end)
end
function disableNoCamCollision()
	State.noCamCollisionEnabled=false; if _noCamConn then _noCamConn:Disconnect(); _noCamConn=nil end
	for p,orig in pairs(_noCamParts) do pcall(function() if p and p.Parent then p.LocalTransparencyModifier=orig end end) end; _noCamParts={}
end

function u2t(u) return {xs=u.X.Scale,xo=u.X.Offset,ys=u.Y.Scale,yo=u.Y.Offset} end
function t2u(t) if not t then return nil end; return UDim2.new(t.xs or 0,t.xo or 0,t.ys or 0,t.yo or 0) end

local saveDebounce=false
-- Saved mobile layout (positions) only written by saveMobileLayout / Save button
local _savedMobileBtns = nil

function saveMobileLayout()
	local mobileBtns = {}
	if _mbBtnRefs then
		for i, btn in ipairs(_mbBtnRefs) do
			if btn then mobileBtns[tostring(i)] = u2t(btn.Position) end
		end
	end
	_savedMobileBtns = mobileBtns
	return mobileBtns
end

function autoSaveConfig(includeMobilePositions)
	if saveDebounce then return end; saveDebounce=true
	task.delay(0.5,function()
		local pos={}
		if _mainFrame then pos.main=u2t(_mainFrame.Position) end
		if _miniFrame then pos.mini=u2t(_miniFrame.Position) end
		if _comboFrame then pos.combo=u2t(_comboFrame.Position) end
		-- Mobile button positions: only when user pressed Save
		if includeMobilePositions then
			pos.mobileBtns = saveMobileLayout()
		elseif _savedMobileBtns then
			pos.mobileBtns = _savedMobileBtns
		elseif type(_loadedConfig)=="table" and type(_loadedConfig.positions)=="table" and _loadedConfig.positions.mobileBtns then
			pos.mobileBtns = _loadedConfig.positions.mobileBtns
		end
		local panelsSave={}; for _,p in ipairs(UserPanels) do table.insert(panelsSave,{name=p.name,code=p.code}) end
		local gpSave={}; for k,v in pairs(GPKeys) do gpSave[k]=v.Name end
		local cfg={
			normalSpeed=State.normalSpeed,carrySpeed=State.carrySpeed,laggerSpeed=State.laggerSpeed,laggerCarrySpeed=State.laggerCarrySpeed,
			speedType=State.speedType,laggerActive=State.laggerActive,
			autoBatKey=Keys.autoBat.Name,speedKey=Keys.speed.Name,laggerKey=Keys.lagger.Name,
			autoStealEnabled=AutoSteal.Enabled,autoStealV2Enabled=AutoSteal.EnabledV2,grabRadius=AutoSteal.Radius,stealDuration=AutoSteal.Duration,
			autoSwitchSpeed=State.autoSwitchSpeed,
			halfFireRange=AutoSteal.HalfFireRange,halfHoldMax=AutoSteal.HalfHoldMax,halfEntryDelay=AutoSteal.HalfEntryDelay,
			infJump=State.infJumpEnabled,infJumpMode=State.infJumpMode,antiRagdoll=State.antiRagdollEnabled,antiDie=State.antiDieEnabled,fpsBoost=State.fpsBoostEnabled,
			medusaCounter=State.medusaCounterEnabled,dropBrainrotKey=Keys.dropBrainrot.Name,
			autoPlayKey=Keys.autoPlay.Name,guiHideKey=Keys.guiHide.Name,
			animEnabled=State.animEnabled,unwalkEnabled=State.unwalkEnabled,tpDownKey=Keys.tpDown.Name,
			hitboxEnabled=State.hitboxEnabled,darkModeEnabled=State.darkModeEnabled,removeAccEnabled=State.removeAccEnabled,
			menuScale=State.menuScale,autoPlaySide=State.autoPlaySide,
			aimbotSpeed=State.aimbotSpeed,autoSwingEnabled=State.autoSwingEnabled,
			panels=panelsSave,positions=pos,lang=LANG,
			autoTPDownEnabled=State.autoTPDownEnabled,jumpThreshold=State.jumpThreshold,
			hardHitEnabled=State.hardHitEnabled,hardHitRadius=State.hardHitRadius,batRadius=State.batRadius,
			antiLagEnabled=State.antiLagEnabled,ultraModeEnabled=State.ultraModeEnabled,
			espEnabled=State.espEnabled,tracerEnabled=State.tracerEnabled,noCamCollisionEnabled=State.noCamCollisionEnabled,
			gpKeys=gpSave,
			aimBypassToggled=State.aimBypassToggled,antiTpDownToggled=State.antiTpDownToggled,antiTpDownSpeed=State.antiTpDownSpeed,
		
		-- NEW: Save mobile button scale
		mobileBtnScale=State.mobileBtnScale,
		mobileBtnSquare=State.mobileBtnSquare,
		mobileBtnBox=State.mobileBtnBox,
		}
		pcall(function() if writefile then writefile(SAVE_FILE,HttpService:JSONEncode(cfg)) end end); saveDebounce=false
	end)
end

function refreshUIToggles()
	if setSpeedToggleUI then setSpeedToggleUI(State.speedType=="carry") end
	if setLaggerToggleUI then setLaggerToggleUI(State.laggerActive) end
	if modeValLbl then if State.laggerActive then modeValLbl.Text=(State.speedType=="normal") and T("lagger_normal_lbl") or T("lagger_carry_lbl") else modeValLbl.Text=(State.speedType=="normal") and T("normal") or T("carry") end end
end
function toggleSpeedType()
	State.speedType=(State.speedType=="normal") and "carry" or "normal"
	if _G.SLAXER_SpeedConfig then
		_G.SLAXER_SpeedConfig.carry = (State.speedType == "carry")
	end
	refreshUIToggles(); autoSaveConfig()
end
function toggleLagger()
	State.laggerActive = not State.laggerActive
	if _G.SLAXER_SpeedConfig then
		_G.SLAXER_SpeedConfig.family = State.laggerActive and "lagger" or "normal"
		_G.SLAXER_SpeedConfig.carry = (State.speedType == "carry")
	end
	refreshUIToggles()
	autoSaveConfig()
end
function getCurrentSpeed()
	if type(_G.SLAXER_GetSpeed) == "function" then
		return _G.SLAXER_GetSpeed()
	end
	if State.laggerActive then return State.speedType=="normal" and State.laggerSpeed or State.laggerCarrySpeed
	else return State.speedType=="normal" and State.normalSpeed or State.carrySpeed end
end
function getAutoMoveSpeed() return State.laggerActive and State.laggerSpeed or State.normalSpeed end


-- Reliable TP Down (works every press)
local _tpDownBusy = false
function tpToGround()
	if _tpDownBusy then return end
	local char = LP.Character
	if not char then return end
	local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not root then return end

	_tpDownBusy = true

	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		local rp = RaycastParams.new()
		rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char}
		rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		rp.IgnoreWater = false

		local origin = root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
		local hit = workspace:Raycast(origin, Vector3.new(0, -5000, 0), rp)

		-- Fallback: cast from higher if nothing under feet
		if not hit then
			hit = workspace:Raycast(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 50, 0), Vector3.new(0, -8000, 0), rp)
		end

		if hit then
			local hip = (hum and hum.HipHeight) or 2
			local off = hip + (root.Size.Y * 0.5)
			local targetY = hit.Position.Y + off
			local look = root.CFrame.LookVector
			root.CFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(root.Position.X, targetY, root.Position.Z), Vector3.new(root.Position.X, targetY, root.Position.Z) + Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z))
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			pcall(function()
				root.Velocity = Vector3.zero
				root.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end)
			if hum then
				pcall(function()
					hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landing)
					hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
				end)
			end
		else
			-- last resort: drop hard downward
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -250, 0)
		end
	end)

	if not ok then
		warn("[SLAXER] tpToGround error:", err)
	end

	-- short cooldown so spam presses still work, but no re-entry race
	task.delay(0.05, function()
		_tpDownBusy = false
	end)
end

local _jumpConn = nil
function startAutoTPDown()
	State.autoTPDownEnabled = true
	if _jumpConn then _jumpConn:Disconnect(); _jumpConn = nil end
	State.jumpCounter = 0
	_jumpConn = UIS.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
		if not State.autoTPDownEnabled then return end
		State.jumpCounter = State.jumpCounter + 1
		if State.jumpCounter >= (State.jumpThreshold or 2) then
			State.jumpCounter = 0
			task.delay(0.1, function() tpToGround() end)
		end
	end)
end
function stopAutoTPDown()
	State.autoTPDownEnabled = false
	if _jumpConn then _jumpConn:Disconnect(); _jumpConn = nil end
	State.jumpCounter = 0
end

function runDropBrainrot()
	-- MANUAL ONLY (button / keybind H) — full drop with ascend then land
	if State.dropBrainrotActive then return end
	local char = LP.Character
	if not char then return end
	local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not root then return end
	State.dropBrainrotActive = true

	-- Pause ANTI DROP briefly so drop works, then restore
	local _hadAntiDrop = false
	pcall(function()
		if type(_G.SLAXER_IsAntiDropOn) == "function" and _G.SLAXER_IsAntiDropOn() then
			_hadAntiDrop = true
			if type(_G.SLAXER_StopAntiDrop) == "function" then _G.SLAXER_StopAntiDrop() end
		end
	end)

	-- ===== إسقاط البرينروت فعلياً =====
	pcall(function()
		-- 1) فك أي Tool مش بات/سلاف
		for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
			if tool:IsA("Tool") then
				local n = tool.Name:lower()
				if not (n:find("bat") or n:find("slap")) then
					pcall(function()
						if hum then hum:UnequipTools() end
					end)
					pcall(function() tool.Parent = workspace end)
					pcall(function()
						local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
						if bp then tool.Parent = bp end
					end)
				end
			end
		end
		-- 2) موديلات/فولدرات محمولة
		for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Model") or child:IsA("Folder") then
				local n = child.Name:lower()
				if n:find("brain") or n:find("animal") or n:find("pet") or n:find("carry") or n:find("steal") then
					pcall(function() child.Parent = workspace end)
				end
			end
		end
		-- 3) فك لحامات الحمل
		for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
			if obj:IsA("Weld") or obj:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
				local n = (obj.Name or ""):lower()
				if n:find("carry") or n:find("brain") or n:find("animal") or n:find("grab") or n:find("hold") or n:find("attach") then
					pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
				end
			end
		end
		-- 4) ريموتات دروب شائعة في Steal a Brainrot
		local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
		local names = {
			"Drop","DropItem","DropBrainrot","DropAnimal","Unequip","Release","Throw",
			"DropPet","DropCarry","ForceDrop","PlayerDrop"
		}
		for _, n in ipairs(names) do
			local r = rs:FindFirstChild(n, true)
			if r then
				if r:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
					pcall(function() r:FireServer() end)
					pcall(function() r:FireServer("Drop") end)
					pcall(function() r:FireServer(true) end)
				elseif r:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
					pcall(function() r:InvokeServer() end)
				end
			end
		end
		-- Packages / Net folders
		for _, folderName in ipairs({"Packages","Net","Remotes","Events","Network"}) do
			local folder = rs:FindFirstChild(folderName)
			if folder then
				for _, r in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
					if r:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
						local ln = r.Name:lower()
						if ln:find("drop") or ln:find("unequip") or ln:find("release") then
							pcall(function() r:FireServer() end)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	-- 5) صعود سريع ثم نزول للأرض (ضروري عشان الدروب يشتغل)
	local t0 = tick()
	local dur = (type(DROP_ASCEND_DURATION) == "number" and DROP_ASCEND_DURATION) or 0.2
	local spd = (type(DROP_ASCEND_SPEED) == "number" and DROP_ASCEND_SPEED) or 150
	local dc
	dc = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		local r = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not r then
			if dc then dc:Disconnect() end
			State.dropBrainrotActive = false
			if _hadAntiDrop then
				task.defer(function()
					pcall(function()
						if type(_G.SLAXER_StartAntiDrop) == "function" then _G.SLAXER_StartAntiDrop() end
					end)
				end)
			end
			return
		end
		if tick() - t0 >= dur then
			if dc then dc:Disconnect() end
			local rp = RaycastParams.new()
			rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char}
			rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
			local rr = workspace:Raycast(r.Position, Vector3.new(0, -2000, 0), rp)
			if rr then
				local hum2 = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local off = (hum2 and hum2.HipHeight or 2) + (r.Size.Y / 2)
				r.CFrame = CFrame.new(r.Position.X, rr.Position.Y + off, r.Position.Z)
				r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				pcall(function() r.Velocity = Vector3.zero end)
			end
			State.dropBrainrotActive = false
			if _hadAntiDrop then
				task.defer(function()
					pcall(function()
						if type(_G.SLAXER_StartAntiDrop) == "function" then _G.SLAXER_StartAntiDrop() end
					end)
				end)
			end
			return
		end
		r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, spd, 0)
		pcall(function() r.Velocity = Vector3.new(r.Velocity.X, spd, r.Velocity.Z) end)
	end)
end

local Anims={idle1="rbxassetid://133806214992291",idle2="rbxassetid://94970088341563",walk="rbxassetid://707897309",run="rbxassetid://707861613",jump="rbxassetid://116936326516985",fall="rbxassetid://116936326516985",climb="rbxassetid://116936326516985",swim="rbxassetid://116936326516985",swimidle="rbxassetid://116936326516985"}
task.spawn(function() pcall(function() ContentProvider:PreloadAsync({Anims.idle1,Anims.idle2,Anims.walk,Anims.run}) end) end)
function isPackAnim(id) if not id then return false end; for _,v in pairs(Anims) do if v==id then return true end end; return false end
function saveOriginalAnims(char)
	local animate=char:FindFirstChild("Animate"); if not animate then return end
	local function g(obj) return obj and obj.AnimationId or nil end
	local ids={idle1=g(animate.idle and animate.idle.Animation1),idle2=g(animate.idle and animate.idle.Animation2),walk=g(animate.walk and animate.walk.WalkAnim),run=g(animate.run and animate.run.RunAnim),jump=g(animate.jump and animate.jump.JumpAnim),fall=g(animate.fall and animate.fall.FallAnim),climb=g(animate.climb and animate.climb.ClimbAnim),swim=g(animate.swim and animate.swim.Swim),swimidle=g(animate.swimidle and animate.swimidle.SwimIdle)}
	if not isPackAnim(ids.walk) then originalAnims=ids end
end
function applyAnimPack(char)
	local animate=char:FindFirstChild("Animate"); if not animate then return end
	local function s(obj,id) if obj then obj.AnimationId=id end end
	s(animate.idle and animate.idle.Animation1,Anims.idle1); s(animate.idle and animate.idle.Animation2,Anims.idle2); s(animate.walk and animate.walk.WalkAnim,Anims.walk); s(animate.run and animate.run.RunAnim,Anims.run); s(animate.jump and animate.jump.JumpAnim,Anims.jump); s(animate.fall and animate.fall.FallAnim,Anims.fall); s(animate.climb and animate.climb.ClimbAnim,Anims.climb); s(animate.swim and animate.swim.Swim,Anims.swim); s(animate.swimidle and animate.swimidle.SwimIdle,Anims.swimidle)
end
function restoreOriginalAnims(char)
	if not originalAnims then return end; local animate=char:FindFirstChild("Animate"); if not animate then return end
	local function s(obj,id) if obj and id then obj.AnimationId=id end end
	s(animate.idle and animate.idle.Animation1,originalAnims.idle1); s(animate.idle and animate.idle.Animation2,originalAnims.idle2); s(animate.walk and animate.walk.WalkAnim,originalAnims.walk); s(animate.run and animate.run.RunAnim,originalAnims.run); s(animate.jump and animate.jump.JumpAnim,originalAnims.jump); s(animate.fall and animate.fall.FallAnim,originalAnims.fall); s(animate.climb and animate.climb.ClimbAnim,originalAnims.climb); s(animate.swim and animate.swim.Swim,originalAnims.swim); s(animate.swimidle and animate.swimidle.SwimIdle,originalAnims.swimidle)
	local hum2=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); if hum2 then for _,track in ipairs(hum2:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do track:Stop(0) end; hum2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end
end
function startAnimToggle()
	if animHeartbeatConn then animHeartbeatConn:Disconnect(); animHeartbeatConn=nil end
	local char=LP.Character
	if char then saveOriginalAnims(char); applyAnimPack(char); local hum2=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); if hum2 then for _,track in ipairs(hum2:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do track:Stop(0) end; hum2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end end
	local _animLast=0; animHeartbeatConn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function() if not State.animEnabled then return end; local n=tick(); if n-_animLast<0.35 then return end; _animLast=n; local c=LP.Character; if c then applyAnimPack(c) end end)
end
function stopAnimToggle() if animHeartbeatConn then animHeartbeatConn:Disconnect(); animHeartbeatConn=nil end; local char=LP.Character; if char then restoreOriginalAnims(char) end end
function startUnwalk()
	if State.unwalkEnabled then return end; State.unwalkEnabled=true; local c=LP.Character; if not c then return end
	local hum=c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); if hum then for _,t in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do t:Stop() end end
	local anim=c:FindFirstChild("Animate"); if anim then savedAnimate=anim:Clone(); anim:Destroy() end
end
function stopUnwalk()
	if not State.unwalkEnabled then return end; State.unwalkEnabled=false
	local c=LP.Character; if c and savedAnimate then savedAnimate.Parent=c; savedAnimate.Disabled=false; savedAnimate=nil end
	task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.15); local char=LP.Character; if not char then return end; if State.animEnabled then saveOriginalAnims(char); applyAnimPack(char) else restoreOriginalAnims(char) end end)
end

local HitboxData={}; local hitboxConn=nil
function removeHitbox(player) if HitboxData[player] then pcall(function() if HitboxData[player].box and HitboxData[player].box.Parent then HitboxData[player].box:Destroy() end; if HitboxData[player].bb and HitboxData[player].bb.Parent then HitboxData[player].bb:Destroy() end end); HitboxData[player]=nil end end
function createHitboxForPlayer(player)
	if player==LP then return end; removeHitbox(player); local char=player.Character; if not char then return end; local root=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if not root then return end
	local box=Instance.new("SelectionBox"); box.Name="SLAXERHitbox"; box.Adornee=root; box.Color3=C_ACCENT; box.LineThickness=0.05; box.SurfaceTransparency=0.85; box.SurfaceColor3=C_ACCENT; box.Parent=workspace
	local head=char:FindFirstChild("Head")
	local bb=Instance.new("BillboardGui"); bb.Name="SLAXERDistLabel"; bb.Size=UDim2.new(0,120,0,30); bb.StudsOffset=Vector3.new(0,2.5,0); bb.AlwaysOnTop=true; bb.Adornee=head or root; bb.Parent=workspace
	local distLbl=Instance.new("TextLabel",bb); distLbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); distLbl.BackgroundTransparency=1; distLbl.Text="0m"; distLbl.TextColor3=C_TEXT; distLbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack; distLbl.TextSize=13; distLbl.TextStrokeTransparency=0; distLbl.TextStrokeColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
	HitboxData[player]={box=box,bb=bb,distLbl=distLbl,char=char}
end
function startHitboxes()
	if hitboxConn then return end
	for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p~=LP then createHitboxForPlayer(p) end end
	hitboxConn=Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p) if p==LP then return end; p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(0.5); if State.hitboxEnabled then createHitboxForPlayer(p) end end); task.wait(0.5); if State.hitboxEnabled then createHitboxForPlayer(p) end end)
	for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if p~=LP then p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(0.5); if State.hitboxEnabled then createHitboxForPlayer(p) end end) end end
end
function stopHitboxes() if hitboxConn then hitboxConn:Disconnect(); hitboxConn=nil end; for p,_ in pairs(HitboxData) do removeHitbox(p) end; HitboxData={} end
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	if not State.hitboxEnabled then return end
	local myChar=LP.Character; local myRoot=myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if not myRoot then return end
	for player,data in pairs(HitboxData) do pcall(function()
		if not player.Character then removeHitbox(player); return end
		local theirRoot=player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if not theirRoot then return end
		if not data.box or not data.box.Parent then createHitboxForPlayer(player); return end
		local dist=math.floor((myRoot.Position-theirRoot.Position).Magnitude); if data.distLbl then data.distLbl.Text=dist.."m" end
	end) end
end)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p) removeHitbox(p) end)

function addHeadLabel(char)
	if not char then return end; local head=char:WaitForChild("Head",5); if not head then return end
	local old=head:FindFirstChild("SLAXERHeadLabel"); if old then old:Destroy() end
	local bb=Instance.new("BillboardGui",head); bb.Name="SLAXERHeadLabel"; bb.Size=UDim2.new(0,140,0,30); bb.StudsOffset=Vector3.new(0,1.5,0); bb.AlwaysOnTop=false
	local lbl=Instance.new("TextLabel",bb); lbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); lbl.BackgroundTransparency=1; lbl.Text="PRIME"; lbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack; lbl.TextSize=13; lbl.TextColor3=C_TEXT; lbl.TextStrokeTransparency=0; lbl.TextStrokeColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
end

for _,name in pairs({"SLAXERGUI","SLAXERGUI2"}) do
	pcall(function() local o=game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild(name); if o then o:Destroy() end end)
	pcall(function() local o=LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and LP.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild(name); if o then o:Destroy() end end)
end

function findMedusa()
	local char=LP.Character; if not char then return nil end
	for _,tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do if tool:IsA("Tool") then local tn=tool.Name:lower(); if tn:find("medusa") or tn:find("head") or tn:find("stone") then return tool end end end
	local bp2=LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack"); if bp2 then for _,tool in ipairs(bp2:GetChildren()) do if tool:IsA("Tool") then local tn=tool.Name:lower(); if tn:find("medusa") or tn:find("head") or tn:find("stone") then return tool end end end end
	return nil
end
function useMedusaCounter()
	if State.medusaDebounce then return end; if tick()-State.medusaLastUsed<25 then return end
	local char=LP.Character; if not char then return end; State.medusaDebounce=true
	local med=findMedusa(); if not med then State.medusaDebounce=false; return end
	if med.Parent~=char then local hum2=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); if hum2 then hum2:EquipTool(med) end end
	pcall(function() med:Activate() end); State.medusaLastUsed=tick(); State.medusaDebounce=false
end
function onAnchorChanged(part) return part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Anchored"):Connect(function() if part.Anchored and part.Transparency==1 then useMedusaCounter() end end) end
setupMedusaCounter=function(char)
	stopMedusaCounter(); if not char then return end
	for _,part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do if part:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(Conns.anchor,onAnchorChanged(part)) end end
	table.insert(Conns.anchor,char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(part) if part:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(Conns.anchor,onAnchorChanged(part)) end end))
end
stopMedusaCounter=function() for _,c in pairs(Conns.anchor) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end; Conns.anchor={} end

startAutoPlay=function()
	if Conns.autoPlay then Conns.autoPlay:Disconnect() end
	local phase=1
	local posA=State.autoPlaySide=="right" and POS.R1 or POS.L1
	local posB=State.autoPlaySide=="right" and POS.R2 or POS.L2
	local pauseUntil=0
	Conns.autoPlay=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not State.autoPlayEnabled then return end
		-- UI lock must NOT freeze auto play movement
		local char=LP.Character; if not char then return end
		local root=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); local hum2=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); if not root or not hum2 then return end
		if tick() < pauseUntil then return end
		local spd=getAutoMoveSpeed(); local target=phase==1 and posA or posB; local tgt=Vector3.new(target.X,root.Position.Y,target.Z)
		if (tgt-root.Position).Magnitude<1.25 then
			if phase==1 then
				phase=2
				pauseUntil = tick() + 0.1 -- was 0.3
			else
				hum2:Move(Vector3.zero,false)
				root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(0,root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y,0)
				State.autoPlayEnabled=false
				if Conns.autoPlay then Conns.autoPlay:Disconnect(); Conns.autoPlay=nil end
				if setAutoPlayUI then setAutoPlayUI(false) end
			end
			return
		end
		local d=target-root.Position; local flat=Vector3.new(d.X,0,d.Z)
		if flat.Magnitude < 0.05 then return end
		local mv=flat.Unit
		hum2:Move(mv,false)
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(mv.X*spd,root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y,mv.Z*spd)
	end)
end
stopAutoPlay=function()
	if Conns.autoPlay then Conns.autoPlay:Disconnect(); Conns.autoPlay=nil end
	State.autoPlayEnabled=false
	local char=LP.Character
	if char then
		local hum2=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if hum2 then hum2:Move(Vector3.zero,false); hum2.AutoRotate=true end
		local root=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if root then root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(0,root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y,0) end
	end
	if setAutoPlayUI then setAutoPlayUI(false) end
end

-- Anti-Ragdoll: recover from ragdoll WITHOUT locking movement
local _antiRagCooldown = 0
local _antiRagInterval = 0.08
local _antiRagLastRecover = 0
pcall(function()
	local ok, fps = pcall(function()
		return math.floor(1 / math.max(game:GetService("Stats").FrameTime:GetValue(), 0.001))
	end)
	if ok and type(fps) == "number" and fps > 0 and fps < 40 then
		_antiRagInterval = 0.14
	elseif ok and type(fps) == "number" and fps >= 55 then
		_antiRagInterval = 0.06
	end
end)
startAntiRagdoll=function()
	if Conns.antiRag then return end
	Conns.antiRag=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if tick() - _antiRagCooldown < 0.04 then return end
		_antiRagCooldown = tick()
		local char = LP.Character
		if not char then return end
		local hum2 = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hum2 or hum2.Health <= 0 then return end

		if hum2.PlatformStand then hum2.PlatformStand = false end
		hum2:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp, true)
		hum2:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running, true)
		hum2:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
		hum2:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall, true)
		hum2:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed, true)

		local st = hum2:GetState()
		local isRag = (st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
			or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
			or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
			or hum2.PlatformStand)

		if isRag then
			pcall(function()
				hum2.PlatformStand = false
				hum2.Sit = false
				hum2.AutoRotate = true
				hum2:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics, false)
				hum2:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
				hum2:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
				hum2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
				hum2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
			end)
			if workspace.CurrentCamera then
				workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum2
			end
			pcall(function()
				local pm = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
				local mod = pm and pm:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
				local cm = mod and mod:FindFirstChild("ControlModule")
				if cm then
					local ok, m = pcall(require, cm)
					if ok and m and m.Enable then m:Enable() end
				end
			end)
			for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
				if obj:IsA("Motor6D") and not obj.Enabled then
					pcall(function() obj.Enabled = true end)
				end
			end
			if root then
				local v = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
				-- stop extreme fling only (keep normal movement)
				if v.Magnitude > 150 or math.abs(v.Y) > 120 then
					root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
						math.clamp(v.X, -65, 65),
						math.clamp(v.Y, -50, 45),
						math.clamp(v.Z, -65, 65)
					)
				end
				if root.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude > 18 then
					root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = root.AssemblyAngularVelocity * 0.2
				end
				-- allow walking while recovering
				local md = hum2.MoveDirection
				if md.Magnitude > 0.08 then
					local want = md.Unit * 30
					local cur = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
					root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
						cur.X * 0.35 + want.X * 0.65,
						math.clamp(cur.Y, -45, 50),
						cur.Z * 0.35 + want.Z * 0.65
					)
				end
			end
		end
		if hum2.WalkSpeed < 1 then
			hum2.WalkSpeed = 16
		end
	end)
end
stopAntiRagdoll=function() if Conns.antiRag then Conns.antiRag:Disconnect(); Conns.antiRag=nil end end

-- ====================== ANTI DIE (GUI + commands) ======================
-- Logic stays active when GUI is hidden. No fall-velocity clamp (no slow jump).
_antiDieInvUntil = 0
ANTI_DIE_CFG = { healthThreshold = 35, invincibilityFrames = 0.35, autoRevive = true }
-- Anti server-reset (client-side restore; server LoadCharacter cannot be fully blocked)
local _antiDieSavedCF = nil
local _antiDieSavedT = 0
local _antiDieLastChar = nil
local _antiDieResetCount = 0
local _antiDieResetWindow = 0

local _antiDieGui = nil
local _antiDieMain = nil
local _antiDieOpenBtn = nil
local _antiDieStatusLbl = nil
local _antiDieToggleBtn = nil
local _antiDieToggleStroke = nil

antiDieSuperHeal = function(hum)
	if not hum then return end
	local maxH = hum.MaxHealth or 100
	if maxH <= 0 then maxH = 100 end
	if hum.Health >= maxH and hum.Health > 0 then return end
	pcall(function() hum.Health = maxH end)
	_antiDieInvUntil = tick() + (ANTI_DIE_CFG.invincibilityFrames or 0.35)
	pcall(function()
		local char = hum.Parent
		if not char then return end
		for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("NumberValue") then
				local n = child.Name:lower()
				if n:find("health") or n:find("hp") or n:find("life") then child.Value = maxH end
			elseif child:IsA("BoolValue") and child.Name:lower():find("dead") then
				child.Value = false
			end
		end
	end)
end

local function _antiDieSavePose(char)
	if not char then return end
	local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if root then
		_antiDieSavedCF = root.CFrame
		_antiDieSavedT = tick()
	end
end

local function _antiDieRestorePose(char)
	if not char or not _antiDieSavedCF then return end
	if tick() - _antiDieSavedT > 12 then return end
	local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 2)
	local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 2)
	if not root then return end
	pcall(function()
		root.CFrame = _antiDieSavedCF
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
		if hum then
			hum.PlatformStand = false
			hum.Sit = false
			hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
			hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
			local maxH = hum.MaxHealth or 100
			if maxH <= 0 then maxH = 100 end
			hum.Health = maxH
		end
		if workspace.CurrentCamera then
			workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum or root
		end
	end)
end

antiDieTick = function()
	if not State.antiDieEnabled then return end
	local char = LP.Character
	if not char then return end
	local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not hum then return end

	-- hard anti-death / anti-reset prep
	pcall(function()
		hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
		if hum.PlatformStand then hum.PlatformStand = false end
		if hum.Sit then hum.Sit = false end
	end)

	-- always keep health full to reduce server kill->reset
	if hum.Health > 0 and hum.Health < (hum.MaxHealth or 100) then
		antiDieSuperHeal(hum)
	end
	if tick() < _antiDieInvUntil then
		antiDieSuperHeal(hum)
	end
	if hum.Health > 0 and hum.Health <= (ANTI_DIE_CFG.healthThreshold or 35) then
		antiDieSuperHeal(hum)
	end

	-- position restore disabled (user request)

	if ANTI_DIE_CFG.autoRevive and (hum.Health <= 0 or hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead) then
		antiDieSuperHeal(hum)
		pcall(function()
			hum.PlatformStand = false
			hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
			hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
		end)
		-- no teleport back to old place — stay where server put you
		if root then
			pcall(function()
				root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
				root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end)
		end
	end
end

local function antiDieUpdateGuiVisuals()
	if not _antiDieToggleBtn or not _antiDieStatusLbl then return end
	if State.antiDieEnabled then
		_antiDieToggleBtn.Text = "Disable ANTI DIE"
		_antiDieToggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 0, 0)
		if _antiDieToggleStroke then _antiDieToggleStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50) end
		_antiDieStatusLbl.Text = "Status: ✅ ON + Anti Server Reset"
		_antiDieStatusLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)
	else
		_antiDieToggleBtn.Text = "Enable ANTI DIE"
		_antiDieToggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 0, 0)
		if _antiDieToggleStroke then _antiDieToggleStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(120, 0, 0) end
		_antiDieStatusLbl.Text = "Status: ❌ Disabled"
		_antiDieStatusLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 50, 50)
	end
end

local function antiDieShowPanel()
	if _antiDieMain then _antiDieMain.Visible = true end
	if _antiDieGui then _antiDieGui.Enabled = true end
	if _antiDieOpenBtn then _antiDieOpenBtn.Visible = false end
end

local function antiDieHidePanel()
	-- hide UI only — commands stay running if enabled
	if _antiDieMain then _antiDieMain.Visible = false end
	if _antiDieOpenBtn then _antiDieOpenBtn.Visible = true end
end

local function antiDieBuildGui()
	if _antiDieGui and _antiDieGui.Parent then return end
	pcall(function()
		local old = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and LP.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("SlaxerAntiDieGui")
		if old then old:Destroy() end
		local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
		local o2 = cg:FindFirstChild("SlaxerAntiDieGui")
		if o2 then o2:Destroy() end
	end)

	local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	sg.Name = "SlaxerAntiDieGui"
	sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
	sg.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
	sg.DisplayOrder = 120
	pcall(function() sg.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
	if not sg.Parent then sg.Parent = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
	_antiDieGui = sg

	-- floating reopen button (visible when panel hidden)
	local openBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	openBtn.Name = "AntiDieOpen"
	openBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 110, 0, 36)
	openBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0.55, 0)
	openBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 0, 0)
	openBtn.Text = "ANTI DIE"
	openBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	openBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	openBtn.TextSize = 13
	openBtn.Visible = false
	openBtn.ZIndex = 200
	openBtn.Parent = sg
	Instance.new("UICorner", openBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
	local obs = Instance.new("UIStroke", openBtn)
	obs.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
	obs.Thickness = 1.5
	_antiDieOpenBtn = openBtn

	local main = Instance.new("Frame")
	main.Name = "Main"
	main.Size = UDim2.new(0, 280, 0, 170)
	main.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -140, 0.35, 0)
	main.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 0, 0)
	main.BorderSizePixel = 0
	main.Visible = true
	main.Parent = sg
	Instance.new("UICorner", main).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
	_antiDieMain = main

	local top = Instance.new("Frame")
	top.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
	top.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 0, 0)
	top.BorderSizePixel = 0
	top.Parent = main
	Instance.new("UICorner", top).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

	local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0)
	title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0)
	title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	title.Text = "🛡️ SLAXER ANTI DIE"
	title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	title.TextSize = 15
	title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	title.Parent = top

	local hideBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	hideBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 32)
	hideBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -68, 0, 2)
	hideBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	hideBtn.Text = "−"
	hideBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
	hideBtn.TextSize = 20
	hideBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	hideBtn.Parent = top

	local closeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	closeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 32)
	closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 2)
	closeBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	closeBtn.Text = "✕"
	closeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100)
	closeBtn.TextSize = 18
	closeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	closeBtn.Parent = top

	local status = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	status.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 22)
	status.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 48)
	status.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	status.Text = "Status: ❌ Disabled"
	status.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 50, 50)
	status.TextSize = 13
	status.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
	status.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	status.Parent = main
	_antiDieStatusLbl = status

	local info = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	info.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 18)
	info.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 72)
	info.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	info.Text = "Hide keeps commands ON · ✕ turns OFF"
	info.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 120, 120)
	info.TextSize = 11
	info.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	info.Parent = main

	local toggle = Instance.new("TextButton")
	toggle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 240, 0, 40)
	toggle.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -120, 0, 100)
	toggle.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 0, 0)
	toggle.Text = "Enable ANTI DIE"
	toggle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	toggle.TextSize = 14
	toggle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	toggle.AutoButtonColor = false
	toggle.Parent = main
	Instance.new("UICorner", toggle).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
	local tStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", toggle)
	tStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(120, 0, 0)
	tStroke.Thickness = 1.5
	_antiDieToggleBtn = toggle
	_antiDieToggleStroke = tStroke

	local tip = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	tip.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 18)
	tip.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -22)
	tip.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	tip.Text = "RightShift hide/show panel"
	tip.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 100, 100)
	tip.TextSize = 11
	tip.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	tip.Parent = main

	-- drag
	local dragging, dragInput, dragStart, startPos
	top.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging = true
			dragStart = input.Position
			startPos = main.Position
			input.Changed:Connect(function()
				if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end
			end)
		end
	end)
	top.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragInput = input
		end
	end)
	UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if input == dragInput and dragging and startPos and dragStart then
			local delta = input.Position - dragStart
			main.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
		end
	end)

	toggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if State.antiDieEnabled then
			stopAntiDie()
		else
			startAntiDie()
		end
		-- sync Main tab toggle visual only (avoid re-entrancy)
		pcall(function() if setAntiDie then setAntiDie(State.antiDieEnabled) end end)
		antiDieUpdateGuiVisuals()
		autoSaveConfig()
	end)

	hideBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		antiDieHidePanel() -- commands stay if enabled
	end)

	closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		-- close = turn off commands + hide
		if State.antiDieEnabled then
			stopAntiDie()
			autoSaveConfig()
		end
		pcall(function() if setAntiDie then setAntiDie(false) end end)
		antiDieHidePanel()
		antiDieUpdateGuiVisuals()
	end)

	openBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		antiDieShowPanel()
	end)

	-- drag open button
	do
		local d, di, ds, sp = false, nil, nil, nil
		openBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				d = true; ds = input.Position; sp = openBtn.Position
				input.Changed:Connect(function() if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then d = false end end)
			end
		end)
		UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
			if d and ds and sp and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
				local delta = input.Position - ds
				if delta.Magnitude > 8 then
					openBtn.Position = UDim2.new(sp.X.Scale, sp.X.Offset + delta.X, sp.Y.Scale, sp.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	if not Conns.antiDieKey then
		Conns.antiDieKey = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
			if gp then return end
			if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightShift then
				if _antiDieMain and _antiDieMain.Visible then
					antiDieHidePanel()
				else
					antiDieShowPanel()
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	antiDieUpdateGuiVisuals()
end

startAntiDie = function(silent)
	-- silent=true: keep commands after server reset WITHOUT showing GUI
	local alreadyOn = State.antiDieEnabled == true
	State.antiDieEnabled = true
	antiDieBuildGui()
	if not silent then
		antiDieShowPanel()
	else
		-- stay hidden after reset; only floating button if user had panel closed
		if _antiDieMain then _antiDieMain.Visible = false end
		-- do not force open button either (user may want fully silent)
		if _antiDieOpenBtn and not alreadyOn then
			_antiDieOpenBtn.Visible = false
		end
	end
	antiDieUpdateGuiVisuals()

	if Conns.antiDie then Conns.antiDie:Disconnect(); Conns.antiDie = nil end
	if Conns.antiDieHealth then pcall(function() Conns.antiDieHealth:Disconnect() end); Conns.antiDieHealth = nil end

	Conns.antiDie = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if State.antiDieEnabled then pcall(antiDieTick) end
	end)

	local function bindHealth(char)
		if not char then return end
		local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 2)
		if not hum then return end
		pcall(function() hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false end)
		if Conns.antiDieHealth then pcall(function() Conns.antiDieHealth:Disconnect() end) end
		Conns.antiDieHealth = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
			if not State.antiDieEnabled then return end
			if hum.Health <= (ANTI_DIE_CFG.healthThreshold or 35) or hum.Health <= 0 then
				antiDieSuperHeal(hum)
			end
		end)
		if Conns.antiDieDied then pcall(function() Conns.antiDieDied:Disconnect() end) end
		Conns.antiDieDied = hum.Died:Connect(function()
			if not State.antiDieEnabled then return end
			antiDieSuperHeal(hum)
			_antiDieSavePose(char)
			pcall(function()
				hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
				hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
			end)
		end)
		antiDieSuperHeal(hum)
		_antiDieSavePose(char)
	end
	bindHealth(LP.Character)

	-- ANTI SERVER RESET: on remove save pose, on new char restore + heal hard
	if Conns.antiDieRemoving then pcall(function() Conns.antiDieRemoving:Disconnect() end) end
	Conns.antiDieRemoving = LP.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function(char)
		if not State.antiDieEnabled then return end
		_antiDieSavePose(char)
		local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if hum then
			pcall(function()
				hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
				hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth or 100
			end)
		end
		local now = tick()
		if now - _antiDieResetWindow > 5 then
			_antiDieResetWindow = now
			_antiDieResetCount = 0
		end
		_antiDieResetCount = _antiDieResetCount + 1
	end)

	if Conns.antiDieChar then pcall(function() Conns.antiDieChar:Disconnect() end) end
	Conns.antiDieChar = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
		if not State.antiDieEnabled then return end
		-- silent rebind after server reset (NO GUI, NO position restore)
		task.spawn(function()
			local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or c:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 2)
			bindHealth(c)
			for i = 1, 10 do
				if not State.antiDieEnabled then break end
				if LP.Character ~= c then break end
				if hum then
					pcall(function()
						hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
						hum.PlatformStand = false
						hum.Sit = false
					end)
					antiDieSuperHeal(hum)
				end
				task.wait(0.05)
			end
			if State.antiDieEnabled and not Conns.antiDie then
				startAntiDie(true)
			end
		end)
	end)
end

stopAntiDie = function()
	State.antiDieEnabled = false
	if Conns.antiDie then Conns.antiDie:Disconnect(); Conns.antiDie = nil end
	if Conns.antiDieHealth then pcall(function() Conns.antiDieHealth:Disconnect() end); Conns.antiDieHealth = nil end
	if Conns.antiDieDied then pcall(function() Conns.antiDieDied:Disconnect() end); Conns.antiDieDied = nil end
	if Conns.antiDieChar then pcall(function() Conns.antiDieChar:Disconnect() end); Conns.antiDieChar = nil end
	if Conns.antiDieRemoving then pcall(function() Conns.antiDieRemoving:Disconnect() end); Conns.antiDieRemoving = nil end
	_antiDieInvUntil = 0
	antiDieUpdateGuiVisuals()
	if _antiDieMain then _antiDieMain.Visible = false end
	if _antiDieOpenBtn then _antiDieOpenBtn.Visible = true end
end




-- ====================== ANTI CRASHER (SERVER CHARACTER RESET) ======================
-- Soft-protect against server-forced character resets / crash respawns
local _antiCrashLastHealth = 100
local _antiCrashLastChar = nil
local _antiCrashSavedCF = nil
local _antiCrashSavedT = 0
local _antiCrashResetCount = 0
local _antiCrashResetWindow = 0
local _antiCrashCharRemovingConn = nil
local _antiCrashCharAddedConn = nil

local function _antiCrashSavePose(char)
	if not char then return end
	local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if root then
		_antiCrashSavedCF = root.CFrame
		_antiCrashSavedT = tick()
	end
end

local function _antiCrashRestorePose(char)
	if not char or not _antiCrashSavedCF then return end
	if tick() - _antiCrashSavedT > 8 then return end -- too old
	local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 3)
	local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 3)
	if not root then return end
	pcall(function()
		root.CFrame = _antiCrashSavedCF
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
		if hum then
			hum.PlatformStand = false
			hum.Sit = false
			hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
		end
		if workspace.CurrentCamera then
			workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum or root
		end
	end)
end

function startAntiCrasher()
	if Conns.antiCrash then return end
	State.antiCrasherEnabled = true
	_antiCrashLastChar = nil
	_antiCrashResetCount = 0
	_antiCrashResetWindow = tick()

	Conns.antiCrash = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not State.antiCrasherEnabled then return end
		local char = LP.Character
		if not char then return end
		local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hum then return end

		if _antiCrashLastChar ~= char then
			_antiCrashLastChar = char
			_antiCrashLastHealth = hum.Health
			-- after server reset: restore last pose
			task.defer(function()
				task.wait(0.05)
				if State.antiCrasherEnabled and LP.Character == char then
					_antiCrashRestorePose(char)
				end
			end)
		end

		-- continuously save safe pose when stable
		if root and hum.Health > 0 then
			local v = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
			if v.Magnitude < 90 then
				_antiCrashSavePose(char)
			end
		end

		pcall(function()
			hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
			if hum.PlatformStand then hum.PlatformStand = false end
			if hum.Sit then hum.Sit = false end
			hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp, true)
			hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running, true)
			hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
		end)

		if root then
			pcall(function()
				-- extreme spin can trigger server phys issues / soft reset
				if root.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude > 80 then
					root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end
			end)
		end

		if hum.Health > 0 then
			_antiCrashLastHealth = hum.Health
		end
	end)

	if not _G._SLAXER_AntiCrashCharHook then
		_G._SLAXER_AntiCrashCharHook = true

		_antiCrashCharRemovingConn = LP.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function(char)
			if not State.antiCrasherEnabled then return end
			_antiCrashSavePose(char)
			local now = tick()
			if now - _antiCrashResetWindow > 6 then
				_antiCrashResetWindow = now
				_antiCrashResetCount = 0
			end
			_antiCrashResetCount = _antiCrashResetCount + 1
			-- rapid server reset spam detected
			if _antiCrashResetCount >= 3 then
				warn("[SLAXER] ANTI CRASHER: rapid server character resets detected")
			end
		end)

		_antiCrashCharAddedConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
			if not State.antiCrasherEnabled then return end
			task.spawn(function()
				task.wait(0.08)
				_antiCrashRestorePose(char)
				task.wait(0.25)
				_antiCrashRestorePose(char)
			end)
		end)
	end
end

function stopAntiCrasher()
	State.antiCrasherEnabled = false
	if Conns.antiCrash then
		Conns.antiCrash:Disconnect()
		Conns.antiCrash = nil
	end
end




applyFPSBoost=function()
	pcall(function() if setfpscap then setfpscap(999999999) end end)
	local function processObj(v) pcall(function()
		if v:IsA("Model") then v.LevelOfDetail=Enum.ModelLevelOfDetail.Disabled; v.ModelStreamingMode=Enum.ModelStreamingMode.Nonatomic
		elseif v:IsA("MeshPart") then v.CastShadow=false; v.DoubleSided=false; v.RenderFidelity=Enum.RenderFidelity.Performance
		elseif v:IsA("BasePart") then v.CastShadow=false; v.Material=Enum.Material.Plastic; v.Reflectance=0
		elseif v:IsA("Decal") or v:IsA("Texture") then v.Transparency=1
		elseif v:IsA("SpecialMesh") then v.TextureId=""
		elseif v:IsA("Fire") or v:IsA("SpotLight") or v:IsA("Smoke") or v:IsA("Sparkles") or v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Trail") or v:IsA("Beam") then v.Enabled=false
		elseif v:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") or v:IsA("MaterialVariant") then v:Destroy()
		elseif v:IsA("Attachment") then v.Visible=false end
	end) end
	for _,v in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do processObj(v) end
	pcall(function()
		local l=game:GetService("Lighting")
		for _,v in pairs(l:GetDescendants()) do pcall(function() if v:IsA("Sky") or v:IsA("Atmosphere") or v:IsA("BloomEffect") or v:IsA("BlurEffect") or v:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or v:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") or v:IsA("Clouds") or v:IsA("PostEffect") or v:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") then v:Destroy() end end) end
		pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(l,"Technology",Enum.Technology.Legacy) end)
		l.GlobalShadows=false; l.FogEnd=9e9; l.Brightness=0
		local terrain=workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain"); if terrain then pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(terrain,"Decoration",false) end); terrain.WaterReflectance=0; terrain.WaterTransparency=0.7; terrain.WaterWaveSize=0; terrain.WaterWaveSpeed=0 end
	end)
end

local _playerCache, _playerCacheT = {}, 0
local function getPlayersCached()
	local n = tick()
	if n - _playerCacheT > 0.2 then
		_playerCacheT = n
		_playerCache = Players:GetPlayers()
	end
	return _playerCache
end
local VYSE_HIT_DIST=10; local SWING_COOLDOWN=0.012; local FACE_OFFSET=1.0; local SIDE_OFFSET=2.0; local ORBIT_SPEED=6.0
local BAT_SLAP_LIST={"Bat","Slap","Iron Slap","Gold Slap","Diamond Slap","Emerald Slap","Ruby Slap","Dark Matter Slap","Flame Slap","Nuclear Slap","Galaxy Slap","Glitched Slap"}

function getBat()
	local char=LP.Character; if not char then return nil end
	for _,name in ipairs(BAT_SLAP_LIST) do local t=char:FindFirstChild(name); if t and t:IsA("Tool") then return t end end
	for _,ch in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("Tool") and ch.Name:lower():find("bat") then return ch end end
	local bp=LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack"); if bp then
		for _,name in ipairs(BAT_SLAP_LIST) do local t=bp:FindFirstChild(name); if t and t:IsA("Tool") then local hum=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); if hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(t) end) end; return t end end
		for _,ch in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("Tool") and ch.Name:lower():find("bat") then local hum=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"); if hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(ch) end) end; return ch end end
	end
	return nil
end

-- Hit - stronger register
function tryHitBat()
	if State.hittingCooldown then return end
	State.hittingCooldown = true
	pcall(function()
		local bat = getBat()
		if not bat then return end
		local char = LP.Character
		local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if hum and bat.Parent ~= char then
			pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end)
			task.wait()
		end
		-- force activate many times
		for _ = 1, 18 do
			pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
		end
		pcall(function()
			if bat:IsA("Tool") then
				local a = bat:FindFirstChild("Activate") or bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
			end
		end)
		for _, obj in ipairs(bat:GetDescendants()) do
			if obj:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
				pcall(function() obj:FireServer() end)
				pcall(function() obj:FireServer(true) end)
				pcall(function() obj:FireServer(1) end)
				pcall(function() obj:FireServer(LP.Character) end)
			elseif obj:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
				pcall(function() obj:InvokeServer() end)
			end
		end
		local handle = bat:FindFirstChild("Handle") or bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
		local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if handle and hrp then
			for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if p ~= LP and p.Character then
					local tr = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if tr and (hrp.Position - tr.Position).Magnitude < 28 then
						for _, pn in ipairs({"HumanoidRootPart", "UpperTorso", "Torso", "Head", "LowerTorso", "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "LeftUpperArm", "RightUpperArm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg"}) do
							local part = p.Character:FindFirstChild(pn)
							if part then
								pcall(function()
									if firetouchinterest then
										firetouchinterest(handle, part, 0)
										firetouchinterest(handle, part, 1)
									end
								end)
								pcall(function()
									if firetouchinterest then
										firetouchinterest(part, handle, 0)
										firetouchinterest(part, handle, 1)
									end
								end)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
	task.delay(0.012, function() State.hittingCooldown = false end)
	_G._SLAXER_BatHitStabilizeUntil = tick() + 0.12
end

-- أفضل استهداف: أقرب + أمام الكاميرا + sticky أطول
local _autoBatLockP, _autoBatLockT = nil, 0
function getClosestPlayerAim()
	local char = LP.Character
	if not char then return nil, math.huge, nil end
	local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not root then return nil, math.huge, nil end
	local partsList = {"HumanoidRootPart", "UpperTorso", "Torso", "Head", "LowerTorso", "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "LeftUpperArm", "RightUpperArm"}
	local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
	local camLook = Vector3.new(root.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, root.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
	if cam then
		local cl = Vector3.new(cam.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, cam.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
		if cl.Magnitude > 0.05 then camLook = cl.Unit
		elseif camLook.Magnitude > 0.05 then camLook = camLook.Unit
		else camLook = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
	elseif camLook.Magnitude > 0.05 then
		camLook = camLook.Unit
	else
		camLook = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
	end

	local function bodyPart(pchar, tr, d)
		local nearestPart, nearestD = tr, d
		for _, partName in ipairs(partsList) do
			local part = pchar:FindFirstChild(partName)
			if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
				local pd = (root.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
				if pd < nearestD then nearestD = pd; nearestPart = part end
			end
		end
		return nearestPart
	end

	-- sticky: keep target if still valid and not too far
	if _autoBatLockP and tick() < _autoBatLockT then
		local p = _autoBatLockP
		if p.Parent and p.Character then
			local ph = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local tr = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if tr and ph and ph.Health > 0 then
				local d = (root.Position - tr.Position).Magnitude
				if d < 100 then
					return p, d, bodyPart(p.Character, tr, d)
				end
			end
		end
		_autoBatLockP = nil
	end

	local closest, bestScore, bestPart, bestDist = nil, -1e9, nil, math.huge
	for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if p ~= LP and p.Character then
			local ph = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local tr = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if tr and ph and ph.Health > 0 then
				local offset = tr.Position - root.Position
				local d = offset.Magnitude
				if d < 150 then
					local flat = Vector3.new(offset.X, 0, offset.Z)
					local faceScore = 0
					if flat.Magnitude > 0.4 then
						faceScore = camLook:Dot(flat.Unit)
					end
					-- strong preference for closest + in front of view
					local score = (150 - d) * 1.4 + faceScore * 70
					if d < 8 then score = score + 50
					elseif d < 18 then score = score + 20 end
					-- prefer targets roughly same height (not under map)
					local yDiff = math.abs(offset.Y)
					if yDiff < 8 then score = score + 15
					elseif yDiff > 40 then score = score - 25 end
					if score > bestScore then
						bestScore = score
						bestDist = d
						closest = p
						bestPart = bodyPart(p.Character, tr, d)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if closest then
		_autoBatLockP = closest
		_autoBatLockT = tick() + 0.75
	end
	return closest, bestDist, bestPart
end

function startBatAimbot()
	if Conns.aimbot then Conns.aimbot:Disconnect() end
	local orbitAngle = (tick() * 1.7) % (math.pi * 2)
	local _batKnockUntil = 0
	Conns.aimbot = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		if not State.autoBatToggled then return end
		local char = LP.Character
		if not char then return end
		local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not root then return end
		local hum2 = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not hum2 or hum2.Health <= 0 then return end
		local st = hum2:GetState()
		if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then return end

		if _G._SLAXER_BatHitStabilizeUntil and tick() < _G._SLAXER_BatHitStabilizeUntil then
			local mv = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
			if mv.Magnitude > 60 then
				root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(mv.X * 0.4, math.clamp(mv.Y, -35, 25), mv.Z * 0.4)
			end
			root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
		end
		-- after being slapped: damp velocity + pause chase so you stay in control
		local myVel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
		local mag = myVel.Magnitude
		if mag > 100 then
			_batKnockUntil = math.max(_batKnockUntil, tick() + 0.25)
		elseif mag > 80 then
			_batKnockUntil = math.max(_batKnockUntil, tick() + 0.12)
		end
		if tick() < _batKnockUntil then
			-- light damp only (keep control without freezing you)
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(myVel.X * 0.55, math.clamp(myVel.Y, -45, 30), myVel.Z * 0.55)
			root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			pcall(function()
				hum2.PlatformStand = false
				hum2.AutoRotate = true
			end)
			return
		end
		-- soft cap extreme only
		if mag > 100 then
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = myVel.Unit * 70
		end
		if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
			or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
			or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
			_batKnockUntil = math.max(_batKnockUntil, tick() + 0.4)
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(myVel.X * 0.3, math.clamp(myVel.Y, -25, 20), myVel.Z * 0.3)
			root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			pcall(function()
				hum2.PlatformStand = false
				hum2:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
			end)
			return
		end

		if not char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
			local bat = getBat()
			if bat then pcall(function() hum2:EquipTool(bat) end) end
		end

		local target, dist, aimPart = getClosestPlayerAim()
		if not target or not target.Character or not aimPart then
			pcall(function() root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
			hum2.AutoRotate = true
			return
		end

		local tr = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not tr then return end

		local targetVel = tr.AssemblyLinearVelocity
		local myPos = root.Position
		local targetPos = tr.Position
		local aimPos = (aimPart and aimPart.Position) or targetPos

		local spd = targetVel.Magnitude
		local predictTime = 0.10
		if spd > 40 then predictTime = 0.15
		elseif spd > 20 then predictTime = 0.12 end
		local predictPos = targetPos + Vector3.new(targetVel.X, targetVel.Y * 0.2, targetVel.Z) * predictTime

		-- orbit like original (fast turn + stick)
		local oSpeed = (ORBIT_SPEED or 6.0) * 1.0
		orbitAngle = orbitAngle + (dt or 0.016) * oSpeed
		if orbitAngle > math.pi * 2 then orbitAngle = orbitAngle - math.pi * 2 end

		local dist = (myPos - targetPos).Magnitude
		-- tighter orbit = actually lands hits
		local radius = 0.85
		if dist > 25 then radius = 1.6
		elseif dist > 12 then radius = 1.2
		elseif dist < 6 then radius = 0.55 end

		local ox = math.cos(orbitAngle) * radius
		local oz = math.sin(orbitAngle) * radius
		local oy = math.sin(orbitAngle * 2.0) * 0.55
		local orbitPos = predictPos + Vector3.new(ox, oy, oz)

		local toOrbit = orbitPos - myPos
		local flat = Vector3.new(toOrbit.X, 0, toOrbit.Z)

		-- fixed 56.5
		local chaseSpeed = 56.5
		if tonumber(State.aimbotSpeed) and State.aimbotSpeed > 0 then
			chaseSpeed = math.min(math.clamp(tonumber(State.aimbotSpeed), 1, 56.5), 56.5)
		end

		local yVel = math.clamp(toOrbit.Y * 8, -35, 38)

		local desiredVel
		if flat.Magnitude > 0.2 then
			local d = flat.Unit
			desiredVel = Vector3.new(d.X * chaseSpeed, yVel, d.Z * chaseSpeed)
		else
			local tang = Vector3.new(-math.sin(orbitAngle), 0, math.cos(orbitAngle))
			desiredVel = Vector3.new(
				targetVel.X * 0.6 + tang.X * chaseSpeed * 0.5,
				yVel,
				targetVel.Z * 0.6 + tang.Z * chaseSpeed * 0.5
			)
		end
		local alpha = dist < 12 and 0.65 or 0.55
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Lerp(desiredVel, alpha)

		-- fast turn like original
		hum2.AutoRotate = false
		local lookDir = Vector3.new(targetPos.X - myPos.X, 0, targetPos.Z - myPos.Z)
		if lookDir.Magnitude > 0.12 then
			lookDir = lookDir.Unit
			local currentLook = Vector3.new(root.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, root.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
			if currentLook.Magnitude > 0.1 then currentLook = currentLook.Unit end
			local cross = currentLook:Cross(lookDir)
			local dot = math.clamp(currentLook:Dot(lookDir), -1, 1)
			local yaw = math.atan2(cross.Y, dot)
			root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, math.clamp(yaw * 42, -52, 52), 0)
		end

		local realDist = (myPos - targetPos).Magnitude
		local partDist = (myPos - aimPos).Magnitude
		-- always swing when close (ignore autoSwing off for auto bat)
		if realDist <= 26 or partDist <= 24 then
			tryHitBat()
		elseif realDist <= 30 then
			tryHitBat()
		end
	end)
end
function stopBatAimbot()
	if Conns.aimbot then Conns.aimbot:Disconnect(); Conns.aimbot=nil end
	State.hittingCooldown=false
	local char=LP.Character
	local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local hum2=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if root then
		-- نخفف السرعة فقط بدل تصفيرها (التصفير يسبب سقوط/موت أحياناً)
		local v = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X * 0.35, math.clamp(v.Y, -50, 40), v.Z * 0.35)
		root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
	end
	if hum2 then hum2.AutoRotate=true end
end

function _aimV2FindBat()
	local char = LP.Character
	if not char then return nil end
	for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
		if tool:IsA("Tool") then
			local n = tool.Name:lower()
			if n:find("bat") or n:find("slap") then return tool end
		end
	end
	local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	if bp then
		for _, tool in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
			if tool:IsA("Tool") then
				local n = tool.Name:lower()
				if n:find("bat") or n:find("slap") then return tool end
			end
		end
	end
	return nil
end
-- ====================== BAT V2 = RXZ BAT BYPASS logic ======================
local BAT_V2_SPEED = 56.5
local bypassHittingCooldown = false

local function findBatV2()
	local char = LP.Character
	if not char then return nil end
	local bat = char:FindFirstChild("Bat")
	if bat and bat:IsA("Tool") then return bat end
	for _, t in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
		if t:IsA("Tool") then
			local n = t.Name:lower()
			if n:find("bat") or n:find("slap") then return t end
		end
	end
	local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack") or LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
	if bp then
		bat = bp:FindFirstChild("Bat")
		if bat and bat:IsA("Tool") then return bat end
		for _, t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
			if t:IsA("Tool") then
				local n = t.Name:lower()
				if n:find("bat") or n:find("slap") then return t end
			end
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function getClosestPlayerBatV2()
	local char = LP.Character
	if not char then return nil, math.huge end
	local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not root then return nil, math.huge end
	local closest, closestDist = nil, math.huge
	for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if player ~= LP and player.Character then
			local hrp = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local hum = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
				local mag = (root.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
				if mag < closestDist then
					closestDist = mag
					closest = player
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return closest, closestDist
end

local function tryHitBatV2()
	if bypassHittingCooldown then return end
	bypassHittingCooldown = true
	pcall(function()
		local tool = findBatV2()
		if not tool then return end
		local char = LP.Character
		local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if tool.Parent ~= char and hum then
			pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end)
		end
		pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
		local remoteEvent = tool:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
		if remoteEvent then
			pcall(function() remoteEvent:FireServer() end)
		end
	end)
	task.delay(0.08, function()
		bypassHittingCooldown = false
	end)
end

function startAimBypass()
	if Conns.aimBypass then return end
	State.aimBypassToggled = true
	local speed = 56.5
	if State.aimbotSpeed and State.aimbotSpeed > 0 then
		speed = math.clamp(tonumber(State.aimbotSpeed) or 56.5, 1, 56.5)
	end
	BAT_V2_SPEED = speed

	Conns.aimBypass = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if not State.aimBypassToggled then return end
		local char = LP.Character
		if not char then return end
		local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not root then return end

		-- don't ragdoll yourself while bypass is on
		if hum then
			hum.PlatformStand = false
			hum.Sit = false
			local st = hum:GetState()
			if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
				or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
				pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end)
			end
		end

		local targetPlayer, dist = getClosestPlayerBatV2()
		if not targetPlayer or not targetPlayer.Character then return end
		local targetHrp = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not targetHrp then return end
