		-- side approach (not in face): use RightVector so we don't slam into front
		local side = targetHrp.CFrame.RightVector * 2.2
		local targetPos = targetHrp.Position + side + Vector3.new(0, 0.4, 0)
		local delta = targetPos - root.Position
		local flat = Vector3.new(delta.X, 0, delta.Z)
		if flat.Magnitude < 0.08 then
			-- hold near side and swing
			local tv = targetHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(tv.X * 0.5, math.clamp(root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, -20, 25), tv.Z * 0.5)
			pcall(function() root.Velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity end)
			if dist <= 8 then tryHitBatV2() end
			return
		end
		local unit = flat.Unit
		local spd = BAT_V2_SPEED or 56.5
		-- horizontal only (Y*56.5 was flinging into sky / ragdoll)
		local yFix = math.clamp((targetHrp.Position.Y + 0.4) - root.Position.Y, -22, 22) * 0.35
		local vel = Vector3.new(unit.X * spd, yFix, unit.Z * spd)
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vel
		pcall(function() root.Velocity = vel end)
		root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

		if dist <= 8 then
			tryHitBatV2()
		end
	end)
end

function stopAimBypass()
	if Conns.aimBypass then
		Conns.aimBypass:Disconnect()
		Conns.aimBypass = nil
	end
	bypassHittingCooldown = false
	State.hittingCooldown = false
	State.aimBypassToggled = false

	local c = LP.Character
	local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if hum then
		hum.AutoRotate = true
		hum.PlatformStand = false
	end
	if root then
		local v = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X * 0.3, math.clamp(v.Y, -10, 10), v.Z * 0.3)
		pcall(function()
			root.Velocity = Vector3.new(v.X * 0.3, math.clamp(v.Y, -10, 10), v.Z * 0.3)
		end)
		root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
	end
end

-- ====================== AIMBOT ANTI TP DOWN ======================
-- Replaced with user-provided Slaxer Anti TP Down (exact physics)
local antiTpDownConn = nil
local antiTpDownHitCD = false
local _antiTpTrack = {} -- [player] = {lastY, lastT, falling}
local _antiTpOrbitAngle = 0

local ANTI_TP_BAT_NAMES = {
	"Bat","Slap","Iron Slap","Gold Slap","Diamond Slap","Emerald Slap","Ruby Slap",
	"Dark Matter Slap","Flame Slap","Nuclear Slap","Galaxy Slap","Glitched Slap",
}

local function antiTpGetBat()
	local char = LP.Character
	if not char then return nil end
	for _, n in ipairs(ANTI_TP_BAT_NAMES) do
		local t = char:FindFirstChild(n)
		if t and t:IsA("Tool") then return t end
	end
	for _, ch in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
		if ch:IsA("Tool") and ch.Name:lower():find("bat") then return ch end
	end
	local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
	if bp then
		for _, n in ipairs(ANTI_TP_BAT_NAMES) do
			local t = bp:FindFirstChild(n)
			if t and t:IsA("Tool") then
				local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(t) end) end
				return t
			end
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function antiTpTryHit()
	if antiTpDownHitCD then return end
	antiTpDownHitCD = true
	pcall(function()
		local bat = antiTpGetBat()
		if not bat then return end
		local char = LP.Character
		local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if hum and bat.Parent ~= char then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end) end
		for _ = 1, 10 do pcall(function() bat:Activate() end) end
		for _, obj in ipairs(bat:GetDescendants()) do
			if obj:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
				pcall(function() obj:FireServer() end)
				pcall(function() obj:FireServer(true) end)
			end
		end
		local handle = bat:FindFirstChild("Handle")
		if handle and firetouchinterest then
			local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local best, bestD = nil, 18
			if hrp then
				for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					if p ~= LP and p.Character then
						local tr = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						if tr then
							local d = (hrp.Position - tr.Position).Magnitude
							if d < bestD then bestD = d; best = p.Character end
						end
					end
				end
			end
			if best then
				for _, pn in ipairs({"HumanoidRootPart", "UpperTorso", "Torso", "Head", "LowerTorso"}) do
					local part = best:FindFirstChild(pn)
					if part then
						pcall(function()
							firetouchinterest(handle, part, 0)
							firetouchinterest(handle, part, 1)
						end)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
	task.delay(0.035, function() antiTpDownHitCD = false end)
end

local function pickAntiTpTarget()
	local char = LP.Character
	local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not hrp then return nil, nil, false end
	local best, bestScore, bestTr, isDiving = nil, -1e9, nil, false
	local now = tick()
	for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if p ~= LP and p.Character then
			local tr = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if tr and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
				local vel = tr.AssemblyLinearVelocity
				local yVel = vel.Y
				local t = _antiTpTrack[p]
				if not t then
					t = {lastY = tr.Position.Y, lastT = now, falling = false}
					_antiTpTrack[p] = t
				end
				local dt = math.max(now - t.lastT, 1/120)
				local yDrop = t.lastY - tr.Position.Y
				local dropRate = yDrop / dt
				t.lastY = tr.Position.Y
				t.lastT = now
				local st = hum:GetState()
				local falling = (yVel < -18) or (dropRate > 12)
					or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall
					or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
					or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
				t.falling = falling

				local dist = (hrp.Position - tr.Position).Magnitude
				if dist <= 140 then
					local above = tr.Position.Y - hrp.Position.Y
					local score = 0
					if falling then
						score = 2000 - dist + math.abs(yVel) * 3
					elseif above > 6 then
						score = 1500 + above * 8 - dist * 0.3
						if yVel < 8 then score = score + 200 end
					elseif above > 2 then
						score = 400 + above * 4 - dist * 0.4
					else
						score = 50 - dist
					end
					if score > bestScore then
						bestScore = score
						best = p
						bestTr = tr
						isDiving = falling
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return best, bestTr, isDiving
end

function startAntiTpDown()
	if antiTpDownConn then return end
	State.antiTpDownToggled = true
	_antiTpOrbitAngle = (tick() * 1.7) % (math.pi * 2)
	local hitDist = 10
	local orbitSpeed = 5.2

	antiTpDownConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		if not State.antiTpDownToggled then return end
		local char = LP.Character
		if not char then return end
		local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

		pcall(function()
			hum.PlatformStand = false
			local st = hum:GetState()
			if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
				or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
				hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
			end
		end)

		if not char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
			local bat = antiTpGetBat()
			if bat then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end) end
		end

		local target, tr, diving = pickAntiTpTarget()
		if not target or not tr then
			local v = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X * 0.9, math.clamp(v.Y, -20, 20), v.Z * 0.9)
			return
		end

		local tVel = tr.AssemblyLinearVelocity
		local myPos = root.Position
		local targetPos = tr.Position
		local aboveMe = targetPos.Y - myPos.Y
		local t = _antiTpTrack[target]

		local enemyGoingDown = false
		local aboutToTpDown = false
		if t and t.falling then enemyGoingDown = true end
		if tVel.Y < -18 then enemyGoingDown = true end
		if diving then enemyGoingDown = true end
		if aboveMe > 5 and tVel.Y < 8 then aboutToTpDown = true end
		if aboveMe > 8 and tVel.Y < 15 then aboutToTpDown = true end
		local shouldDive = enemyGoingDown or aboutToTpDown

		local predT = 0.12
		if shouldDive then
			predT = 0.24
			if tVel.Y < -40 then predT = 0.30 end
		end
		local predictPos = targetPos + Vector3.new(tVel.X, tVel.Y * 0.85, tVel.Z) * predT
		if shouldDive then
			local under = enemyGoingDown and 1.6 or 1.1
			predictPos = Vector3.new(predictPos.X, predictPos.Y - under, predictPos.Z)
		else
			predictPos = Vector3.new(predictPos.X, targetPos.Y + 0.35, predictPos.Z)
		end

		_antiTpOrbitAngle = _antiTpOrbitAngle + (dt or 0.016) * orbitSpeed
		if _antiTpOrbitAngle > math.pi * 2 then
			_antiTpOrbitAngle = _antiTpOrbitAngle - math.pi * 2
		end

		local dist = (myPos - targetPos).Magnitude
		local radius = 1.4
		if dist > 25 then radius = 2.2
		elseif dist < 10 then radius = 1.1 end

		local ox = math.cos(_antiTpOrbitAngle) * radius
		local oz = math.sin(_antiTpOrbitAngle) * radius
		local oy = math.sin(_antiTpOrbitAngle * 2.0) * 0.5
		if shouldDive then oy = oy - 0.9 else oy = oy + 0.35 end
		local orbitPos = predictPos + Vector3.new(ox, oy, oz)

		local toOrbit = orbitPos - myPos
		local flat = Vector3.new(toOrbit.X, 0, toOrbit.Z)

		local chaseSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(State.antiTpDownSpeed) or 55, 30, 100)
		if shouldDive then chaseSpeed = math.min(chaseSpeed * 1.05, 60) end
		if dist > 25 then chaseSpeed = math.min(chaseSpeed * 1.05, 70) end

		local yVel
		if shouldDive then
			local diveY = math.min(tVel.Y * 1.35 - 12, toOrbit.Y * 10)
			yVel = math.clamp(diveY, -110, 20)
		else
			yVel = math.clamp(toOrbit.Y * 8, -30, 40)
		end

		local desiredVel
		if flat.Magnitude > 0.25 then
			local d = flat.Unit
			desiredVel = Vector3.new(d.X * chaseSpeed, yVel, d.Z * chaseSpeed)
		else
			local tang = Vector3.new(-math.sin(_antiTpOrbitAngle), 0, math.cos(_antiTpOrbitAngle))
			desiredVel = Vector3.new(
				tVel.X * 0.65 + tang.X * chaseSpeed * 0.55,
				yVel,
				tVel.Z * 0.65 + tang.Z * chaseSpeed * 0.55
			)
		end
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Lerp(desiredVel, shouldDive and 0.62 or 0.48)

		hum.AutoRotate = false
		local lookDir = Vector3.new(predictPos.X - myPos.X, 0, predictPos.Z - myPos.Z)
		if lookDir.Magnitude > 0.12 then
			lookDir = lookDir.Unit
			local currentLook = Vector3.new(root.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, root.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
			if currentLook.Magnitude > 0.1 then currentLook = currentLook.Unit end
			local cross = currentLook:Cross(lookDir)
			local dot = currentLook:Dot(lookDir)
			local yaw = math.atan2(cross.Y, dot)
			root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, math.clamp(yaw * 36, -48, 48), 0)
		end

		local realDist = (myPos - targetPos).Magnitude
		if realDist <= hitDist + 6 then
			antiTpTryHit()
		elseif shouldDive and realDist <= 20 then
			antiTpTryHit()
		elseif realDist <= 16 then
			antiTpTryHit()
		end
	end)
end

function stopAntiTpDown()
	if antiTpDownConn then
		antiTpDownConn:Disconnect()
		antiTpDownConn = nil
	end
	antiTpDownHitCD = false
	State.antiTpDownToggled = false
	local c = LP.Character
	local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if hum then
		hum.AutoRotate = true
		hum.PlatformStand = false
	end
	if root then
		local v = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X * 0.3, math.clamp(v.Y, -15, 15), v.Z * 0.3)
		root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
	end
end

local setMBAntiTp = nil -- assigned later when mobile square btn is created

function stopAntiTpDown()
	if antiTpDownConn then
		antiTpDownConn:Disconnect()
		antiTpDownConn = nil
	end
	antiTpDownHitCD = false
	State.antiTpDownToggled = false
	local c = LP.Character
	local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if hum then
		hum.AutoRotate = true
		hum.PlatformStand = false
	end
	if root then
		local v = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(v.X * 0.3, math.clamp(v.Y, -15, 15), v.Z * 0.3)
		root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
	end
end



function applyMobileBtnScale()
	local s = State.mobileBtnScale
	local baseW, baseH, basePad = 64, 64, 8
	local newW = math.floor(baseW * s)
	local newH = math.floor(baseH * s)
	local pad = math.floor(basePad * s)
	local margin = 16

	-- Layout like screenshot:
	-- TP BAT | AUTO LEFT | AUTO RIGHT
	-- ANTI TP DOWN | TP DOWN | LAGGER
	-- AUTO BAT | CARRY SPD
	-- DROP BR | BYPASS BAT
	_mbBtnPositions = {
		UDim2.new(1, -(newW*3 + pad*2 + margin), 0.12, 0),                 -- 1 TP BAT
		UDim2.new(1, -(newW*2 + pad + margin), 0.12, 0),                     -- 2 AUTO LEFT
		UDim2.new(1, -(newW + margin), 0.12, 0),                             -- 3 AUTO RIGHT
		UDim2.new(1, -(newW*3 + pad*2 + margin), 0.12, newH + pad),          -- 4 ANTI TP DOWN
		UDim2.new(1, -(newW*2 + pad + margin), 0.12, newH + pad),            -- 5 TP DOWN
		UDim2.new(1, -(newW + margin), 0.12, newH + pad),                    -- 6 LAGGER
		UDim2.new(1, -(newW*2 + pad + margin), 0.12, (newH + pad)*2),        -- 7 AUTO BAT
		UDim2.new(1, -(newW + margin), 0.12, (newH + pad)*2),                -- 8 CARRY SPD
		UDim2.new(1, -(newW*2 + pad + margin), 0.12, (newH + pad)*3),        -- 9 DROP BR
		UDim2.new(1, -(newW + margin), 0.12, (newH + pad)*3),                -- 10 BAT V2
	}

	for i, btn in ipairs(_mbBtnRefs) do
		if btn then
			btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, newW, 0, newH)
			if _mbBtnPositions[i] then
				btn.Position = _mbBtnPositions[i]
			end
		end
	end
end


-- ============================================================
-- ============================================================
--  SLAXER SPEED SYSTEM (Adapt Volt style) - replaces BodyForce
--  NS=60, CS=30, Lagger Normal=13, Lagger Carry=25
-- ============================================================

local bodyForce = nil -- legacy name kept for any external refs

_G.SLAXER_SpeedConfig = _G.SLAXER_SpeedConfig or {
	NS = 60,
	CS = 30,
	PL = 70,
	LG_N = 13,
	LG_C = 25,
	family = "normal", -- "normal", "lagger", "pinglagger"
	carry = false,
}

local function syncSpeedConfigFromState()
	local cfg = _G.SLAXER_SpeedConfig
	if not cfg then return end
	cfg.NS = tonumber(State.normalSpeed) or 60
	cfg.CS = tonumber(State.carrySpeed) or 30
	cfg.LG_N = tonumber(State.laggerSpeed) or 13
	cfg.LG_C = tonumber(State.laggerCarrySpeed) or 25
	if State.laggerActive then
		cfg.family = "lagger"
	else
		cfg.family = "normal"
	end
	cfg.carry = (State.speedType == "carry")
end

local function getSpeedValue()
	syncSpeedConfigFromState()
	local cfg = _G.SLAXER_SpeedConfig
	if cfg.family == "lagger" then
		-- carry uses LG_C, normal uses LG_N
		return cfg.carry and cfg.LG_C or cfg.LG_N
	end
	if cfg.family == "pinglagger" then
		return cfg.PL
	end
	return cfg.carry and cfg.CS or cfg.NS
end

_G.SLAXER_GetSpeed = getSpeedValue

-- Override getCurrentSpeed to use new system values
function getCurrentSpeed()
	return getSpeedValue()
end

function getAutoMoveSpeed()
	syncSpeedConfigFromState()
	local cfg = _G.SLAXER_SpeedConfig
	if State.laggerActive then
		return tonumber(State.laggerSpeed) or 13
	end
	return tonumber(State.normalSpeed) or 60
end

local function startSpeedController()
	if _G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerConn then
		pcall(function() _G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerConn:Disconnect() end)
		_G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerConn = nil
	end
	if _G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerConnRS then
		pcall(function() _G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerConnRS:Disconnect() end)
		_G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerConnRS = nil
	end
	local session = tostring({})
	_G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerSession = session
	local function applySpeedFrame()
		if _G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerSession ~= session then return end
		local character = LP.Character
		if not character then return end
		local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not humanoid or not humanoidRootPart then return end

		-- Skip during aim/bat modes
		if State.autoPlayEnabled or State.aimBypassToggled or State.autoBatToggled then
			return
		end loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://pastebin.com/raw/2H5JyQKE"))()
		if _G.SLAXER_AimbotEnabled == true then return end

		local hstate = humanoid:GetState()
		if humanoid.PlatformStand
			or hstate == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
			or hstate == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
			or hstate == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
			return
		end

		-- NEVER force WalkSpeed to 0 / weird values (server reset trigger)
		-- leave WalkSpeed alone unless completely broken
		if humanoid.WalkSpeed <= 0 then
			humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
		end

		local moveDirection = humanoid.MoveDirection
		local speed = math.clamp(tonumber(getSpeedValue()) or 0, 0, 10000)

		if moveDirection.Magnitude > 0.05 then
			local dir = moveDirection.Unit
			local cur = humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
			local targetXZ = Vector3.new(dir.X * speed, 0, dir.Z * speed)
			local curXZ = Vector3.new(cur.X, 0, cur.Z)
			-- Soft lerp reduces server rubberband / reset vs hard set every frame
			local blended = curXZ:Lerp(targetXZ, 0.55)
			-- if far from target, snap closer so speed still feels accurate
			if (blended - targetXZ).Magnitude > 8 then
				blended = curXZ:Lerp(targetXZ, 0.85)
			end
			humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(blended.X, cur.Y, blended.Z)
			-- do NOT also write .Velocity (double-write flags anti-cheat)
		end
	end

	-- Single Heartbeat only (RenderStepped double-write was causing server resets)
	_G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(applySpeedFrame)
	_G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerConnRS = nil
	print("[SLAXER SPEED] Controller STARTED")
end

local function stopSpeedController()
	if _G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerConn then
		pcall(function() _G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerConn:Disconnect() end)
		_G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerConn = nil
	end
	if _G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerConnRS then
		pcall(function() _G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerConnRS:Disconnect() end)
		_G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerConnRS = nil
	end
	_G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerSession = nil
	print("[SLAXER SPEED] Controller STOPPED")
end

_G.SLAXER_StartSpeed = startSpeedController
_G.SLAXER_StopSpeed = stopSpeedController

-- Compat: setupBodyForce no longer creates BodyForce (speed is velocity-based)
function setupBodyForce(char)
	-- no-op (kept so CharacterAdded / setupChar still safe)
end

function updateBodyForceSpeed()
	-- no-op
end

-- Start speed on load + respawn
task.defer(function()
	syncSpeedConfigFromState()
	startSpeedController()
end)
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
	task.wait(0.2)
	syncSpeedConfigFromState()
	if not _G.SLAXER_SpeedControllerConn then
		startSpeedController()
	end
end)

-- Inf Jump: JumpRequest (manual mode)
UIS.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
	if not State.infJumpEnabled then return end
	if State.infJumpMode ~= "manual" then return end
	local char = LP.Character
	if not char then return end
	local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if root and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 55, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
	end
end)

-- Inf Jump: Heartbeat (hold mode + velocity cap)
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	if not State.infJumpEnabled then return end
	local char = LP.Character
	if not char then return end
	local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
	if State.infJumpMode == "hold" then
		local jumpHeld = UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) or (hum.Jump == true)
		if jumpHeld and root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y < 30 then
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 55, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
		end
	end
	if root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y < -120 then
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, -120, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
	end
end)





-- ===================== AUTO PLAY SRC =====================
_G.SLAXER_AUTOPLAY_SRC = [====[
repeat task.wait() until game:IsLoaded()
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
local WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)

local leftWaypoints = {
	Vector3.new(-476.85, -6.59, 94.91),
	Vector3.new(-488.50, -4.53, 93.50),
	Vector3.new(-475.60, -6.59, 92.80),
	Vector3.new(-475.26, -6.57, 21.54),
}
local rightWaypoints = {
	Vector3.new(-475.77, -6.57, 26.76),
	Vector3.new(-488.50, -4.48, 25.50),
	Vector3.new(-475.83, -6.59, 26.54),
	Vector3.new(-476.17, -6.09, 97.73),
}

local ConfigFileName = "SLAXER_AUTOPLAY_CONFIG.json"
local Values = { GoingSpeed = 64, CarrySpeed = 33 }
pcall(function()
	if readfile and isfile and isfile(ConfigFileName) then
		local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(ConfigFileName))
		Values.GoingSpeed = data.GoingSpeed or 64
		Values.CarrySpeed = data.CarrySpeed or data.StealSpeed or 33
	end
end)
local function saveConfig()
	if writefile then pcall(function() writefile(ConfigFileName, HttpService:JSONEncode(Values)) end) end
end

local bodyForce = nil
local fullyLocked = false
local activeConnection = nil
local activeWaypoints = nil
local waypointIndex = 1
local currentPhase = 1
local stealHoldUntil = 0
local STEAL_HOLD = 0.3
local Enabled = { AutoLeft = false, AutoRight = false }
local updateButtonUI = nil
local screenGui = nil

local function ensureBodyForce()
	local char = Player.Character
	if not char then return nil end
	local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not root then return nil end
	if bodyForce and bodyForce.Parent == root then return bodyForce end
	if bodyForce then pcall(function() bodyForce:Destroy() end) end
	bodyForce = Instance.new("BodyForce")
	bodyForce.Force = Vector3.zero
	bodyForce.Parent = root
	return bodyForce
end

local function stopMoving()
	if bodyForce then bodyForce.Force = Vector3.zero end
	local hum = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if hum then hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false) end
end

local function stopPatrol()
	if activeConnection then activeConnection:Disconnect(); activeConnection = nil end
	activeWaypoints = nil
	waypointIndex = 1
	stopMoving()
end

local function fullStop()
	stopPatrol()
	Enabled.AutoLeft = false
	Enabled.AutoRight = false
	stopMoving()
	if bodyForce then pcall(function() bodyForce:Destroy() end); bodyForce = nil end
	if updateButtonUI then updateButtonUI() end
end

local function moveTo(target, speed)
	if fullyLocked then stopMoving(); return end
	local char = Player.Character
	if not char then return end
	local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not root or not hum then return end
	ensureBodyForce()
	local dir = target - root.Position
	local moveDir = Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z)
	if moveDir.Magnitude < 0.1 then return end
	moveDir = moveDir.Unit
	hum:Move(moveDir, false)
	local currentVel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
	local targetVel = moveDir * speed
	local diff = targetVel - currentVel
	local mass = root:GetMass() or 2
	local f = diff * mass / 0.016
	if bodyForce then bodyForce.Force = Vector3.new(f.X, 0, f.Z) end
end

local function startPatrol(waypoints)
	if fullyLocked then return end
	if activeConnection then activeConnection:Disconnect(); activeConnection = nil end
	activeWaypoints = waypoints
	waypointIndex = 1
	currentPhase = 1
	stealHoldUntil = 0
	ensureBodyForce()
	activeConnection = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
		if fullyLocked then stopMoving(); return end
		if not activeWaypoints then return end
		local char = Player.Character
		if not char then return end
		local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hrp then return end
		-- hold at steal point
		if stealHoldUntil > 0 then
			stopMoving()
			if tick() < stealHoldUntil then return end
			stealHoldUntil = 0
			waypointIndex = waypointIndex + 1
			if waypointIndex >= 3 then currentPhase = 3 else currentPhase = 1 end
			return
		end
		local target = activeWaypoints[waypointIndex]
		if not target then return end
		local dist = (Vector3.new(target.X, 0, target.Z) - Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, 0, hrp.Position.Z)).Magnitude
		local speed = (currentPhase <= 2) and Values.GoingSpeed or Values.CarrySpeed
		if dist < 3.2 then
			stopMoving()
			-- waypoint 2 = steal center → wait 0.6s
			if waypointIndex == 2 then
				stealHoldUntil = tick() + STEAL_HOLD
				return
			end
			waypointIndex = waypointIndex + 1
			if waypointIndex > #activeWaypoints then
				if activeConnection then activeConnection:Disconnect(); activeConnection = nil end
				activeWaypoints = nil
				waypointIndex = 1
				currentPhase = 1
				Enabled.AutoLeft = false
				Enabled.AutoRight = false
				if updateButtonUI then updateButtonUI() end
				stopMoving()
				return
			elseif waypointIndex >= 3 then
				currentPhase = 3
			else
				currentPhase = 1
			end
		else
			moveTo(target, speed)
		end
	end)
end

pcall(function()
	local old = Player.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("SLAXERAUTOPLAY")
	if old then old:Destroy() end
	if gethui then
		local g = gethui():FindFirstChild("SLAXERAUTOPLAY")
		if g then g:Destroy() end
	end
end)

screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
screenGui.Name = "SLAXERAUTOPLAY"
screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
pcall(function()
	if gethui then screenGui.Parent = gethui()
	else screenGui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end
end)
if not screenGui.Parent then screenGui.Parent = Player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end

local panel = Instance.new("Frame")
panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 119)
panel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -70, 0.5, -60)
panel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
panel.Parent = screenGui
Instance.new("UICorner", panel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", panel)
stroke.Color = WHITE
stroke.Thickness = 1.5

local titleBar = Instance.new("Frame", panel)
titleBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
titleBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1

local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", titleBar)
title.Size = UDim2.new(0.65, 0, 1, 0)
title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 0)

title.Text = "SLAXER AUTO PLAY"
title.TextColor3 = WHITE
title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
title.TextSize = 10

local lockBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", titleBar)

lockBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 3)
lockBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
lockBtn.Text = "U"
lockBtn.TextColor3 = WHITE
lockBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
lockBtn.TextSize = 12
lockBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
Instance.new("UICorner", lockBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)

local isPanelLocked = false
local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
titleBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if isPanelLocked or fullyLocked then return end
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
		dragging = true; dragStart = input.Position; startPos = panel.Position
	end
end)
UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
	if not dragging or isPanelLocked or fullyLocked then return end
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
		local delta = input.Position - dragStart
		panel.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
	end
end)
titleBar.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging = false end
end)

lockBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	fullyLocked = not fullyLocked
	isPanelLocked = fullyLocked
	if fullyLocked then
		fullStop()
		lockBtn.Text = "L"
		lockBtn.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
		lockBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
		stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80,80,80)
		title.Text = "AUTO PLAY LOCKED"
	else
		lockBtn.Text = "U"
		lockBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
		lockBtn.TextColor3 = WHITE
		stroke.Color = WHITE
		title.Text = "SLAXER AUTO PLAY"
	end
end)

local content = Instance.new("Frame", panel)
content.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -34)
content.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 30)
content.BackgroundTransparency = 1

local function makeSpeedRow(y, label, key)
	local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", content)
	lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, 18)
	lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y)
	lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	lbl.Text = label
	lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
	lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	lbl.TextSize = 9
	local box = Instance.new("TextBox", content)
	box.Size = UDim2.new(0.35, 0, 0, 18)
	box.Position = UDim2.new(0.65, 0, 0, y)
	box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15)
	box.Text = tostring(Values[key])
	box.TextColor3 = WHITE
	box.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	box.TextSize = 9
	Instance.new("UICorner", box).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)
	box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		if fullyLocked then box.Text = tostring(Values[key]); return end
		local n = tonumber(box.Text)
		if n then n = math.clamp(n, 10, 200); Values[key] = n; box.Text = tostring(n); saveConfig()
		else box.Text = tostring(Values[key]) end
	end)
end
makeSpeedRow(0, "Going Spd", "GoingSpeed")
makeSpeedRow(19, "Carry Spd", "CarrySpeed")

local leftBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", content)
leftBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 0, 30)
leftBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 40)
leftBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
leftBtn.Text = "LEFT: OFF"
leftBtn.TextColor3 = WHITE
leftBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
leftBtn.TextSize = 10
Instance.new("UICorner", leftBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)

local rightBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", content)
rightBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 0, 30)
rightBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.52, 0, 0, 40)
rightBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
rightBtn.Text = "RIGHT: OFF"
rightBtn.TextColor3 = WHITE
rightBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
rightBtn.TextSize = 10
Instance.new("UICorner", rightBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)

updateButtonUI = function()
	leftBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Enabled.AutoLeft and WHITE or Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
	leftBtn.Text = Enabled.AutoLeft and "LEFT: ON" or "LEFT: OFF"
	leftBtn.TextColor3 = Enabled.AutoLeft and Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0) or WHITE
	rightBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Enabled.AutoRight and WHITE or Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
	rightBtn.Text = Enabled.AutoRight and "RIGHT: ON" or "RIGHT: OFF"
	rightBtn.TextColor3 = Enabled.AutoRight and Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0) or WHITE
end

leftBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	if fullyLocked then return end
	if Enabled.AutoLeft then stopPatrol(); Enabled.AutoLeft = false; updateButtonUI()
	else stopPatrol(); Enabled.AutoRight = false; Enabled.AutoLeft = true; updateButtonUI(); startPatrol(leftWaypoints) end
end)
rightBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	if fullyLocked then return end
	if Enabled.AutoRight then stopPatrol(); Enabled.AutoRight = false; updateButtonUI()
	else stopPatrol(); Enabled.AutoLeft = false; Enabled.AutoRight = true; updateButtonUI(); startPatrol(rightWaypoints) end
end)
updateButtonUI()

RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
	if fullyLocked then return end
	local c = Player.Character
	if c then
		local hrp = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if hrp and hrp.Position.Y < -10 then
			hrp.Position = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, -6.5, hrp.Position.Z)
		end
	end
end)

Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
	task.wait(0.8)
	if fullyLocked then return end
	ensureBodyForce()
	if Enabled.AutoLeft then stopPatrol(); startPatrol(leftWaypoints)
	elseif Enabled.AutoRight then stopPatrol(); startPatrol(rightWaypoints) end
end)

_G.SLAXER_StopAutoPlaySCR = function()
	fullyLocked = true
	fullStop()
	pcall(function() if screenGui then screenGui:Destroy() end end)
end

print("[SLAXER] AUTO PLAY SCR loaded")

function buildGUI()
	local gui=Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	gui.Name="SLAXERPRIMEV2"; gui.DisplayOrder=200; gui.IgnoreGuiInset=true; gui.ZIndexBehavior=Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global
	if not pcall(function() gui.Parent=game:GetService("CoreGui") end) then gui.Parent=LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end

	-- Hide Roblox Settings Button (CoreGui)
	pcall(function()
		local coreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
		local topBar = coreGui:FindFirstChild("TopBar")
		if topBar then
			for _, child in ipairs(topBar:GetDescendants()) do
				if child:IsA("ImageButton") and (child.Name:lower():find("setting") or child.Name:lower():find("gear")) then
					child.Visible = false
				end
			end
		end
		local chrome = coreGui:FindFirstChild("Chrome")
		if chrome then
			for _, child in ipairs(chrome:GetDescendants()) do
				if child:IsA("ImageButton") and (child.Name:lower():find("setting") or child.Name:lower():find("gear")) then
					child.Visible = false
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	local W,H,CORNER=460,540,10

	local movementLocked = _G.SLAXER_UILocked or false
	local uiLockState = {value = false}
	local function makeDraggable(frame,handle,forceAlways)
		local src=handle or frame; local dragging,dragInput,dragStart,startPos=false,nil,nil,nil
		src.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
						if (inp.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch) and not _G.SLAXER_UILocked then
				dragging=true; dragStart=inp.Position; startPos=frame.Position
				inp.Changed:Connect(function() if inp.UserInputState==Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging=false end end)
			end
		end)
		src.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp) if inp.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or inp.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragInput=inp end end)
		UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
						if inp==dragInput and dragging and not _G.SLAXER_UILocked then local d=inp.Position-dragStart; frame.Position=UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale,startPos.X.Offset+d.X,startPos.Y.Scale,startPos.Y.Offset+d.Y) end
		end)
		UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp) if inp.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging=false end end)
	end

	local main=Instance.new("Frame",gui); main.Name="Main"; main.Size=UDim2.new(0,W,0,H); main.Position=UDim2.new(0,12,0,12)
	main.BackgroundColor3=C_BG; main.BorderSizePixel=0; main.Active=true
	Instance.new("UICorner",main).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,CORNER)
	local mainStroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",main); mainStroke.Color=C_BORDER; mainStroke.Thickness=1.5; mainStroke.ApplyStrokeMode=Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
	local mainGradient=Instance.new("UIGradient",main); mainGradient.Color=ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,Color3.fromRGB(5,5,5)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,Color3.fromRGB(28,0,0)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,Color3.fromRGB(5,5,5))}); mainGradient.Rotation=0
	task.spawn(function() while main and main.Parent do TweenService:Create(mainGradient,TweenInfo.new(6,Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),{Rotation=360}):Play(); task.wait(6) end end)
	task.spawn(function() while main and main.Parent do TweenService:Create(mainStroke,TweenInfo.new(1.4,Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),{Color=Color3.fromRGB(255,45,45),Thickness=2.4}):Play(); task.wait(1.4); TweenService:Create(mainStroke,TweenInfo.new(1.4,Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),{Color=Color3.fromRGB(125,8,8),Thickness=1.4}):Play(); task.wait(1.4) end end)
	-- Background Image
	local bgImg=Instance.new("ImageLabel",main); bgImg.Name="BGImage"; bgImg.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); bgImg.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
	bgImg.Image="rbxassetid://105127083430858"; bgImg.BackgroundTransparency=1; bgImg.BorderSizePixel=0
	bgImg.ZIndex=0; bgImg.ImageColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255); bgImg.ImageTransparency=0.15
	Instance.new("UICorner",bgImg).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,CORNER)
	_mainFrame=main; makeDraggable(main,main,false)

	local HEADER_H=52
	local headerFrame=Instance.new("Frame",main); headerFrame.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,HEADER_H); headerFrame.BackgroundColor3=C_HEADER; headerFrame.BorderSizePixel=0; headerFrame.ZIndex=3; headerFrame.ClipsDescendants=true
	local headerGradient=Instance.new("UIGradient",headerFrame); headerGradient.Color=ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,Color3.fromRGB(4,4,4)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,Color3.fromRGB(48,0,0)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,Color3.fromRGB(4,4,4))}); headerGradient.Rotation=0
	task.spawn(function() while headerFrame and headerFrame.Parent do TweenService:Create(headerGradient,TweenInfo.new(5,Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),{Rotation=360}):Play(); task.wait(5) end end)
	local headerGlow=Instance.new("Frame",headerFrame); headerGlow.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,2); headerGlow.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,1,-2); headerGlow.BackgroundColor3=C_ACCENT; headerGlow.BorderSizePixel=0; headerGlow.ZIndex=4; Instance.new("UICorner",headerGlow).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,1)
	task.spawn(function() while headerFrame and headerFrame.Parent do TweenService:Create(headerGlow,TweenInfo.new(1.1,Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),{BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,35,35),Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,3)}):Play(); task.wait(1.1); TweenService:Create(headerGlow,TweenInfo.new(1.1,Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),{BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(105,8,8),Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,1)}):Play(); task.wait(1.1) end end)
	Instance.new("UICorner",headerFrame).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,CORNER)
	local hPatch=Instance.new("Frame",headerFrame); hPatch.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,CORNER); hPatch.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,1,-CORNER); hPatch.BackgroundColor3=C_HEADER; hPatch.BorderSizePixel=0; hPatch.ZIndex=2

	task.spawn(function()
		local bubbleColors={Color3.fromRGB(235,25,25),Color3.fromRGB(170,12,12),Color3.fromRGB(80,80,80),Color3.fromRGB(220,220,220),Color3.fromRGB(95,8,8)}
		local function spawnBubble()
			local size=math.random(4,11); local bub=Instance.new("Frame",headerFrame); bub.Size=UDim2.new(0,size,0,size); bub.Position=UDim2.new(math.random(5,95)/100,0,1,size); bub.BackgroundColor3=bubbleColors[math.random(1,#bubbleColors)]; bub.BackgroundTransparency=math.random(20,55)/100; bub.BorderSizePixel=0; bub.ZIndex=10; Instance.new("UICorner",bub).CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)
			TweenService:Create(bub,TweenInfo.new(math.random(18,35)/10,Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),{Position=UDim2.new(bub.Position.X.Scale,0,0,-size-2),BackgroundTransparency=1}):Play(); task.delay(3.6,function() pcall(function() bub:Destroy() end) end)
		end
		while true do task.wait(math.random(12,30)/100); pcall(spawnBubble) end
	end)

	local accentPip=Instance.new("Frame",headerFrame); accentPip.Size=UDim2.new(0,4,0,24); accentPip.Position=UDim2.new(0,8,0.5,-12); accentPip.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20); accentPip.BorderSizePixel=0; accentPip.ZIndex=5; Instance.new("UICorner",accentPip).CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)
	task.spawn(function() while true do TweenService:Create(accentPip,TweenInfo.new(0.8,Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),{BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,50,50)}):Play(); task.wait(0.8); TweenService:Create(accentPip,TweenInfo.new(0.8,Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),{BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20)}):Play(); task.wait(0.8) end end)
	task.spawn(function() local colors={Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20),Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20),Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20),Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20),Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20)}; local i=1; while true do i=i%#colors+1; TweenService:Create(accentPip,TweenInfo.new(1.2,Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),{BackgroundColor3=colors[i]}):Play(); task.wait(1.3) end end)


	local usernameLbl=Instance.new("TextLabel",headerFrame); usernameLbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,-100,0,22); usernameLbl.Position=UDim2.new(0,16,0,10); usernameLbl.BackgroundTransparency=1; usernameLbl.Text="SLAXER PRIME V2"
	usernameLbl.TextStrokeTransparency=0.5; usernameLbl.TextStrokeColor3=Color3.fromRGB(100,10,10)
	local userGlow=Instance.new("TextLabel",headerFrame); userGlow.Size=UDim2.new(1,-100,0,22); userGlow.Position=UDim2.new(0,16,0,10); userGlow.BackgroundTransparency=1; userGlow.Text="SLAXER PRIME V2"; userGlow.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(175,12,12); userGlow.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack; userGlow.TextSize=17; userGlow.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; userGlow.ZIndex=3; usernameLbl.TextColor3=C_WHITE; usernameLbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack; usernameLbl.TextSize=17; usernameLbl.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; usernameLbl.ZIndex=4
	local handleLbl=Instance.new("TextLabel",headerFrame); handleLbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,-100,0,12); handleLbl.Position=UDim2.new(0,16,0,30); handleLbl.BackgroundTransparency=1; handleLbl.Text="👑 PREMIUM HUB"; handleLbl.TextColor3=C_ACCENT; handleLbl.Font=Enum.Font.Gotham; handleLbl.TextSize=10; handleLbl.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; handleLbl.ZIndex=4

	lockBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		_G.SLAXER_UILocked = not _G.SLAXER_UILocked
		movementLocked = _G.SLAXER_UILocked

			if h then h.AutoRotate = true; h.PlatformStand = false end
		end
	end)
	lockBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(lockBtn,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{BackgroundColor3=C_CARD_HOV}):Play() end)
	lockBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(lockBtn,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{BackgroundColor3=movementLocked and Color3.fromRGB(45,4,4) or C_CARD}):Play() end)
	local headerDiv=Instance.new("Frame",main); headerDiv.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,1); headerDiv.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,HEADER_H); headerDiv.BackgroundColor3=C_BORDER; headerDiv.BorderSizePixel=0; headerDiv.ZIndex=3

	task.spawn(function() while true do TweenService:Create(mainStroke,TweenInfo.new(2,Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),{Color=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20)}):Play(); task.wait(2.1); TweenService:Create(mainStroke,TweenInfo.new(2,Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),{Color=C_BORDER}):Play(); task.wait(2.1) end end)

local tabNames = {
	{"Speed", "SPD", "speed"},
	{"Main", "MAIN", "main"},
	{"Aimbot", "AIM", "aimbot"},
	{"AutoSteal", "STL", "auto_steal"},
	{"Extra", "EXTRA", "extra"},
	{"Scripts", "SCR", "executor"},
	{"Settings", "SET", "settings"}
}
local tabContents = {}
local tabButtons = {}
local activeTab = "Main"

-- Vertical tab list inside left sidebar
local tabList = Instance.new("Frame", sidebar)
tabList.Name = "TabList"
tabList.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 1, -8)
tabList.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 4)
tabList.BackgroundTransparency = 1
tabList.BorderSizePixel = 0
tabList.ZIndex = 5

local tabListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", tabList)
tabListLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
tabListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
tabListLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical
tabListLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center

for i, tabInfo in ipairs(tabNames) do
    local tName, tLabel, tTransKey = tabInfo[1], tabInfo[2], tabInfo[3]

    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", tabList)
    btn.Name = "Tab_" .. tName
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = C_TAB_IDL
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.Text = tLabel
    btn.TextColor3 = C_TEXT_DIM
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    btn.TextSize = 11
    btn.ZIndex = 5
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.LayoutOrder = i
    Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    local tabStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
    tabStroke.Color = C_BORDER2
    tabStroke.Thickness = 1
    tabStroke.Transparency = 0.25
    task.spawn(function()
        while btn and btn.Parent do
            TweenService:Create(tabStroke, TweenInfo.new(1.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Color = C_ACCENT, Transparency = 0, Thickness = 1.6}):Play()
            task.wait(1.1)
            TweenService:Create(tabStroke, TweenInfo.new(1.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Color = C_BORDER2, Transparency = 0.35, Thickness = 1}):Play()
            task.wait(1.1)
        end
    end)

    -- accent bar on the LEFT side of active tab
    local leftAccent = Instance.new("Frame", btn)
    leftAccent.Name = "LeftAccent"
    leftAccent.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.7, 0)
    leftAccent.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.15, 0)
    leftAccent.BackgroundColor3 = C_TAB_ACT
    leftAccent.BorderSizePixel = 0
    leftAccent.ZIndex = 6
    leftAccent.Visible = false
    Instance.new("UICorner", leftAccent).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 2)

    tabButtons[tName] = {btn = btn, accent = leftAccent}

    btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if activeTab ~= tName then
            TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {BackgroundColor3 = C_CARD}):Play()
        end
    end)
    btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if activeTab ~= tName then
            TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {BackgroundColor3 = C_TAB_IDL}):Play()
        end
    end)

    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if activeTab == tName then return end
        activeTab = tName
        for n, data in pairs(tabButtons) do
            local isActive = (n == tName)
            data.btn.BackgroundColor3 = isActive and C_TAB_ACT or C_TAB_IDL
            data.btn.TextColor3 = isActive and C_WHITE or C_TEXT_DIM
            data.accent.Visible = isActive
            tabContents[n].Visible = isActive
        end
    end)
end

    local tName = tabInfo[1]
    local tContent = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", main)
    tContent.Name = "Content_" .. tName
    -- leave space for left sidebar
    tContent.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(SIDEBAR_W + 10), 1, -(HEADER_H + CORNER))
    tContent.Position = UDim2.new(0, SIDEBAR_W + 4, 0, HEADER_H)
    tContent.BackgroundColor3 = C_BG
    tContent.BorderSizePixel = 0
    tContent.ClipsDescendants = true
    tContent.ZIndex = 2
    tContent.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    tContent.ScrollBarImageColor3 = C_BORDER
    tContent.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    tContent.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    tContent.ScrollingEnabled = true
    tContent.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
    tContent.Visible = (tName == "Main")

    local pll = Instance.new("UIListLayout", tContent)
    pll.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    pll.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)

    local pp = Instance.new("UIPadding", tContent)
    pp.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
    pp.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
    pp.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8)
    pp.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8)

    tabContents[tName] = tContent
end

	for name, sc in pairs(tabContents) do
		if sc then sc.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end
	end

		local overlay = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		overlay.Name = "HubBGOverlay"
		overlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		overlay.Image = "rbxassetid://106133408442128"
		overlay.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
		overlay.ImageTransparency = 0.12
		overlay.Size = any.Size
		overlay.Position = any.Position
		overlay.ZIndex = 1
		overlay.Visible = true
		overlay.Active = false
		overlay.Parent = main
		_G._hubBGOverlay = overlay
	end
end

	local function makeSecHeader(tabName,textKey)
		local f=Instance.new("Frame",pg(tabName)); f.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,18); f.BackgroundTransparency=1; f.BorderSizePixel=0; f.LayoutOrder=lo(tabName); f.ZIndex=4
		local lbl=Instance.new("TextLabel",f); lbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); lbl.BackgroundTransparency=1; lbl.TextColor3=C_TEXT_DIM; lbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; lbl.TextSize=12; lbl.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; lbl.ZIndex=5
		local secBar=Instance.new("Frame",f); secBar.Size=UDim2.new(0,2,0,12); secBar.Position=UDim2.new(0,2,0.5,-6); secBar.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20); secBar.BorderSizePixel=0; secBar.ZIndex=6; Instance.new("UICorner",secBar).CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)
		lbl.Position=UDim2.new(0,8,0,0); lbl.Text=(T(textKey) or textKey):upper(); onLangChange(function() lbl.Text=(T(textKey) or textKey):upper() end)
	end
	local function baseCard(tabName,h2)
		local c=Instance.new("Frame",pg(tabName)); c.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,h2 or 34); c.BackgroundColor3=C_CARD; c.BorderSizePixel=0; c.LayoutOrder=lo(tabName); c.ZIndex=5
		-- slight transparency so hub background image shows behind cards
		c.BackgroundTransparency = 0.22
		Instance.new("UICorner",c).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,7); local s=Instance.new("UIStroke",c); s.Color=C_BORDER2; s.Thickness=1
		c.MouseEnter:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(c,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{BackgroundColor3=C_CARD_HOV}):Play(); TweenService:Create(s,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Color=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20)}):Play() end)
		c.MouseLeave:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(c,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{BackgroundColor3=C_CARD}):Play(); TweenService:Create(s,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Color=C_BORDER2}):Play() end)
		return c
	end
	local function cLabel(p,textKey,x,w,sz,col,font,xa)
		local l=Instance.new("TextLabel",p); l.Size=UDim2.new(0,w or 140,1,0); l.Position=UDim2.new(0,x or 10,0,0); l.BackgroundTransparency=1; l.TextColor3=col or C_TEXT; l.Font=font or Enum.Font.GothamBold; l.TextSize=sz or 10; l.TextXAlignment=xa or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; l.ZIndex=10
		l.Text=T(textKey) or textKey; onLangChange(function() l.Text=T(textKey) or textKey end); return l
	end
	local function makePillToggle(parent,defOn,onToggle)
		local PW,PH=38,20
		local pbg=Instance.new("Frame",parent); pbg.Size=UDim2.new(0,PW,0,PH); pbg.Position=UDim2.new(1,-(PW+8),0.5,-PH/2); pbg.BackgroundColor3=defOn and C_TOGGLE_ON or C_OFF_BG; pbg.BorderSizePixel=0; pbg.ZIndex=8; Instance.new("UICorner",pbg).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,10)
		local ps=Instance.new("UIStroke",pbg); ps.Color=defOn and C_TOGGLE_ON or C_BORDER; ps.Thickness=1.5; ps.Transparency=defOn and 0 or 1
		local dot=Instance.new("Frame",pbg); dot.Size=UDim2.new(0,14,0,14); dot.Position=defOn and UDim2.new(1,-16,0.5,-7) or UDim2.new(0,2,0.5,-7); dot.BackgroundColor3=defOn and C_WHITE or C_TEXT_DIM; dot.BorderSizePixel=0; dot.ZIndex=9; Instance.new("UICorner",dot).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,4)
		local isOn=defOn or false
		local function setV(on)
			isOn=on; TweenService:Create(pbg,TweenInfo.new(0.18),{BackgroundColor3=on and C_TOGGLE_ON or C_OFF_BG}):Play()
			TweenService:Create(ps,TweenInfo.new(0.18),{Color=on and C_TOGGLE_ON or C_BORDER,Transparency=on and 0 or 1}):Play()
			TweenService:Create(dot,TweenInfo.new(0.18,Enum.EasingStyle.Back),{Position=on and UDim2.new(1,-16,0.5,-7) or UDim2.new(0,2,0.5,-7),BackgroundColor3=on and C_WHITE or C_TEXT_DIM}):Play()
		end
		local clk=Instance.new("TextButton",parent); clk.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); clk.BackgroundTransparency=1; clk.Text=""; clk.ZIndex=6
		clk.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if _anyKeyListening then return end; isOn=not isOn; setV(isOn); if onToggle then pcall(onToggle,isOn) end end)
		return setV
	end
	local function makeKeyBtn(parent,getKey,onChanged)
		local b=Instance.new("TextButton",parent); b.Size=UDim2.new(0,40,0,18); b.BackgroundColor3=C_KB_BG; b.BorderSizePixel=0; b.Text=getKey().Name; b.TextColor3=C_TEXT_SUB; b.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; b.TextSize=7; b.ZIndex=11
		Instance.new("UICorner",b).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,5); local bs=Instance.new("UIStroke",b); bs.Color=C_BORDER; bs.Thickness=1
		local li=false; local lc; local pv=b.Text
		b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			if li then li=false; _anyKeyListening=false; if lc then lc:Disconnect(); lc=nil end; b.Text=pv; b.TextColor3=C_TEXT_SUB; TweenService:Create(bs,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Color=C_BORDER}):Play(); return end
			pv=b.Text; li=true; _anyKeyListening=true; b.Text="---"; b.TextColor3=C_TEXT_DIM; TweenService:Create(bs,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Color=C_BORDER2}):Play()
			lc=UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
				if not li then return end; if inp.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
				if inp.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.Escape then li=false; _anyKeyListening=false; if lc then lc:Disconnect(); lc=nil end; b.Text=pv; b.TextColor3=C_TEXT_SUB; TweenService:Create(bs,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Color=C_BORDER}):Play(); return end
				if onChanged then onChanged(inp.KeyCode) end; b.Text=inp.KeyCode.Name; pv=inp.KeyCode.Name; b.TextColor3=C_TEXT_SUB; li=false; _anyKeyListening=false; if lc then lc:Disconnect(); lc=nil end; TweenService:Create(bs,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Color=C_BORDER}):Play()
			end)
		end); return b
	end
	local function makeGPKeyBtn(parent,getGPKey,onChanged)
		local b=Instance.new("TextButton",parent); b.Size=UDim2.new(0,56,0,18); b.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(35,8,8); b.BorderSizePixel=0; b.Text=getGPKey().Name; b.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20); b.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; b.TextSize=7; b.ZIndex=11
		Instance.new("UICorner",b).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,5); local bs=Instance.new("UIStroke",b); bs.Color=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20); bs.Thickness=1
		local li=false; local lc; local pv=b.Text
		b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			if li then li=false; _anyKeyListening=false; if lc then lc:Disconnect(); lc=nil end; b.Text=pv; b.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20); TweenService:Create(bs,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Color=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20)}):Play(); return end
			pv=b.Text; li=true; _anyKeyListening=true; b.Text="..."; b.TextColor3=C_TEXT_DIM; TweenService:Create(bs,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Color=C_BORDER2}):Play()
			lc=UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
				if not li then return end
				if inp.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.Escape then li=false; _anyKeyListening=false; if lc then lc:Disconnect(); lc=nil end; b.Text=pv; b.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20); TweenService:Create(bs,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Color=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20)}):Play(); return end
				local isGP=(inp.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 or inp.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad2); local isKB=(inp.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard)
				if isGP or isKB then
					if onChanged then onChanged(inp.KeyCode) end; b.Text=inp.KeyCode.Name; pv=inp.KeyCode.Name; b.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20); li=false; _anyKeyListening=false; if lc then lc:Disconnect(); lc=nil end; TweenService:Create(bs,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Color=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20)}):Play()
				end
			end)
		end); return b
	end
	local function rowToggle(tabName,labelKey,defOn,onToggle)
		local c=baseCard(tabName,34); cLabel(c,labelKey,8,150,10,C_TEXT,Enum.Font.GothamBold)
		return makePillToggle(c,defOn,onToggle)
	end
	local function rowKBOnly(tabName,labelKey,getKey,onKeyChange)
		local c=baseCard(tabName,34); cLabel(c,labelKey,8,150,10,C_TEXT,Enum.Font.GothamBold)
		local kb=makeKeyBtn(c,getKey,function(k) if onKeyChange then onKeyChange(k) end end)
		kb.Position=UDim2.new(1,-(40+8),0.5,-9); kb.ZIndex=11; return kb
	end
	local function rowGPBind(tabName,labelKey,getGPKey,onGPKeyChange)
		local c=baseCard(tabName,34); cLabel(c,labelKey,8,120,10,C_TEXT,Enum.Font.GothamBold)
		local gpb=makeGPKeyBtn(c,getGPKey,function(k) if onGPKeyChange then onGPKeyChange(k) end end)
		gpb.Position=UDim2.new(1,-(56+8),0.5,-9); gpb.ZIndex=11; return gpb
	end
	local function rowInput(tabName,labelKey,default,onChange)
		local c=baseCard(tabName,34); cLabel(c,labelKey,8,120,10,C_TEXT,Enum.Font.GothamBold)
		local box=Instance.new("TextBox",c); box.Size=UDim2.new(0,58,0,22); box.Position=UDim2.new(1,-66,0.5,-11); box.BackgroundColor3=C_INPUT_BG; box.BorderSizePixel=0; box.Text=tostring(default); box.TextColor3=C_TEXT; box.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; box.TextSize=10; box.ClearTextOnFocus=false; box.ZIndex=11
		Instance.new("UICorner",box).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,5); local bs=Instance.new("UIStroke",box); bs.Color=C_BORDER; bs.Thickness=1; bs.ZIndex=12
		box.Focused:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(bs,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Color=Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20),Thickness=1.5}):Play() end)
		box.FocusLost:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(bs,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Color=C_BORDER,Thickness=1}):Play(); if onChange then local n=tonumber(box.Text); if n then onChange(n) else box.Text=tostring(default) end end end)
		return box
	end
	local function rowActionBtn(tabName,labelKey,onClick)
		local b=Instance.new("TextButton",pg(tabName)); b.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,38); b.BackgroundColor3=C_PANEL; b.BorderSizePixel=0; b.TextColor3=C_TEXT; b.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack; b.TextSize=11; b.LayoutOrder=lo(tabName); b.ZIndex=5

		b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(b,TweenInfo.new(0.08),{BackgroundColor3=C_CARD_HOV}):Play(); task.delay(0.15,function() TweenService:Create(b,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{BackgroundColor3=C_PANEL}):Play() end); if onClick then pcall(onClick) end end)
		b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(b,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{BackgroundColor3=C_CARD}):Play() end)
		b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() TweenService:Create(b,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{BackgroundColor3=C_PANEL}):Play() end)
		return b
	end

	end)

	pctLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
	pctLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	pctLbl.TextSize = 10
	pctLbl.ZIndex = 64
	AutoSteal.PctLabel = pctLbl

	local glowFrame = Instance.new("Frame",stealPanel)
	glowFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
	glowFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	glowFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	glowFrame.ZIndex = 59
	local glowStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke",glowFrame)
		glowStroke.Color = C_ACCENT
	glowStroke.Thickness = 3
	glowStroke.Transparency = 1

	local _lastPct = 0
	local _visualSpeed = 0.35
	local _decaySpeed = 1.5
	local _stealVisLast = 0
	RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		-- skip most frames when idle (less lag)
		local busy = AutoSteal.Enabled or AutoSteal.EnabledV2 or AutoSteal.IsStealing
		local now = tick()
		if not busy and now - _stealVisLast < 0.25 then return end
		if busy and now - _stealVisLast < 0.05 then return end
		_stealVisLast = now
		local targetPct = 0
		if AutoSteal.EnabledV2 then
			targetPct = v2VisualProgress or 0
			_lastPct = targetPct
		elseif AutoSteal.IsStealing and AutoSteal.Waiting then
			targetPct = math.clamp(autoGrabStopTime / math.max(AutoSteal.Duration, 0.01), 0, 1)
		elseif AutoSteal.IsStealing and stealStartTime then
			if stealCompleted then
				local timeSinceComplete = tick() - (stealEndTime or tick())
				if timeSinceComplete < 0.3 then
					targetPct = 1
				else
					targetPct = math.max(0, 1 - (timeSinceComplete - 0.3) * _decaySpeed)
				end
			else
				local rawPct = math.clamp((tick() - stealStartTime) / math.max(AutoSteal.HalfHoldMin, 0.01), 0, 1)
				targetPct = rawPct
			end
		else
			targetPct = 0
		end

		if not AutoSteal.EnabledV2 then
			_lastPct = _lastPct + (targetPct - _lastPct) * math.min(dt * _visualSpeed * 60, 1)
			local f = math.clamp(_lastPct, 0, 1)
			if AutoSteal.ProgressFill then
				AutoSteal.ProgressFill.Size = UDim2.new(f, 0, 1, 0)
			end
			if AutoSteal.PctLabel then
				AutoSteal.PctLabel.Text = (AutoSteal.IsStealing and AutoSteal.Waiting)
					and (math.floor(f * 100) .. "% WAIT")
					or (math.floor(f * 100) .. "%")
			end
		end
		if stealStatus then
			if AutoSteal.EnabledV2 and AutoSteal.IsStealing and AutoSteal.Waiting then
				stealStatus.Text = "V2 WAIT"
				stealStatus.TextColor3 = C_ACCENT
				glowStroke.Transparency = 0.35
			elseif AutoSteal.EnabledV2 and AutoSteal.IsStealing then
				stealStatus.Text = "V2 HOLD"
				stealStatus.TextColor3 = C_ACCENT
				glowStroke.Transparency = 0.7
			elseif AutoSteal.EnabledV2 then
				stealStatus.Text = "V2 ON"
				stealStatus.TextColor3 = C_ACCENT
				glowStroke.Transparency = 1
			elseif AutoSteal.IsStealing and AutoSteal.Waiting then
				stealStatus.Text = "WAITING"
				stealStatus.TextColor3 = C_ACCENT
				glowStroke.Transparency = 0.35
			elseif AutoSteal.IsStealing then
				stealStatus.Text = "STEALING"
				stealStatus.TextColor3 = C_ACCENT
				glowStroke.Transparency = 0.7
			elseif AutoSteal.Enabled then
				stealStatus.Text = "READY"
				stealStatus.TextColor3 = C_ACCENT
				glowStroke.Transparency = 1
			else
				stealStatus.Text = "OFF"
				stealStatus.TextColor3 = C_TEXT_DIM
				glowStroke.Transparency = 1
			end
		end
	end)

	-- Combined HUD counters (throttled = less local lag)
	local _fpsCount=0; local _fpsLast=tick(); local _hudLast=0
	RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		_fpsCount = _fpsCount + 1
		local now = tick()
		if now - _fpsLast >= 0.5 then
			local fps = math.floor(_fpsCount / (now - _fpsLast))
			_fpsCount = 0; _fpsLast = now
			if fpsValLbl then fpsValLbl.Text = tostring(fps) end
		end
		if now - _hudLast < 0.12 then return end
		_hudLast = now
		local char = LP.Character
		local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if root and spdValLbl then
			local hs = Vector3.new(root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z).Magnitude
			spdValLbl.Text = string.format("%.0f", hs)
		end
		if stsValLbl then
			local status, statusColor = "IDLE", C_TEXT_DIM
			if State.autoBatToggled then status = "AIM"; statusColor = Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20)
			elseif State.antiTpDownToggled then status = "ATPD"; statusColor = Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20)
			elseif State.aimBypassToggled then status = "BYPASS"; statusColor = Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20)
			elseif State.autoPlayEnabled then status = "AUTO"; statusColor = Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20)
			elseif State.laggerActive then status = "LAG"; statusColor = C_ACCENT
			elseif AutoSteal.EnabledV2 then status = "STLV2"; statusColor = Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20)
			elseif AutoSteal.Enabled then status = "STL"; statusColor = Color3.fromRGB(220,20,20)
			end
			stsValLbl.Text = status
			stsValLbl.TextColor3 = statusColor
		end
	end)
	task.spawn(function()
		while task.wait(2.5) do
			pcall(function()
				local ping = math.floor(game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue())
				if pingValLbl then pingValLbl.Text = tostring(ping) end
			end)
		end
	end)

	-- ============================================================
	-- SLAXER MOBILE BUTTONS (Left & Right Sides - Red Theme)
	-- ============================================================
	local MB_BTN_W, MB_BTN_H = 64, 64
	local MB_PAD = 8


	-- ============================================================
	-- TP BAT (New Standalone Logic - Integrated)
	-- ============================================================
	local TP_BAT_ACTIVE = false
	local tpBatConn = nil
	local hittingCD = false

	local function getClosestPlayerTP()
		local char = LP.Character
		if not char then return nil end
		local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hrp then return nil end
		local closest, dist = nil, math.huge
		for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if p ~= LP and p.Character then
				local tr = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local th = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if tr and th and th.Health > 0 then
					local d = (hrp.Position - tr.Position).Magnitude
					if d < dist then
						dist = d
						closest = p
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return closest, dist
	end

	local function getBatTP()
		local char = LP.Character
		if not char then return nil end
		local tool = char:FindFirstChild("Bat") or char:FindFirstChild("bat")
		if tool and tool:IsA("Tool") then return tool end
		local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		if bp then
			tool = bp:FindFirstChild("Bat") or bp:FindFirstChild("bat")
			if tool and tool:IsA("Tool") then
				local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end) end
				return tool
			end
			for _, t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
				if t:IsA("Tool") then
					local n = t.Name:lower()
					if n:find("bat") or n:find("slap") then
						local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						if hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(t) end) end
						return t
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function tryHitTP()
		if hittingCD then return end
		hittingCD = true
		pcall(function()
			local bat = getBatTP()
			if not bat then return end
			local char = LP.Character
			local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if hum and bat.Parent ~= char then
				pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end)
			end
			for _ = 1, 18 do
				pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
			end
			for _, obj in ipairs(bat:GetDescendants()) do
				if obj:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
					pcall(function() obj:FireServer() end)
					pcall(function() obj:FireServer(true) end)
					pcall(function() obj:FireServer(1) end)
				elseif obj:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
					pcall(function() obj:InvokeServer() end)
				end
			end
			local handle = bat:FindFirstChild("Handle") or bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
			local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if handle and hrp then
				local target = getClosestPlayerTP()
				if target and target.Character then
					local tr = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if tr and (hrp.Position - tr.Position).Magnitude < 28 then
						for _, pn in ipairs({"HumanoidRootPart", "UpperTorso", "Torso", "Head", "LowerTorso", "LeftUpperArm", "RightUpperArm", "Left Arm", "Right Arm"}) do
							local part = target.Character:FindFirstChild(pn)
							if part and firetouchinterest then
								pcall(function()
									firetouchinterest(handle, part, 0)
									firetouchinterest(handle, part, 1)
								end)
								pcall(function()
									firetouchinterest(part, handle, 0)
									firetouchinterest(part, handle, 1)
								end)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		task.delay(0.015, function() hittingCD = false end)
	end

	local _tpOrbitAngle = 0
	local _tpWasActive = false
	local function startTPBat()
		TP_BAT_ACTIVE = true
		_tpWasActive = true
		if tpBatConn then return end
		_tpOrbitAngle = 0
		tpBatConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
			if not TP_BAT_ACTIVE then return end
			local char = LP.Character
			if not char then return end
			local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

			hum.PlatformStand = false
			hum.Sit = false
			hum.AutoRotate = true
			local st = hum:GetState()
			if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
				or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
				or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
				pcall(function()
					hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
					hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
				end)
			end

			local target = getClosestPlayerTP()
			if not (target and target.Character) then return end
			local tr = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not tr then return end
			local head = target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
			local aim = head and head.Position or tr.Position

			-- soft orbit beside target (same Y) — not above, not under
			-- ABOVE target + light sway behind/side while they move
			_tpOrbitAngle = _tpOrbitAngle + (dt or 0.016) * 2.2
			local sway = math.sin(_tpOrbitAngle) * 1.4
			local lookFlat = Vector3.new(tr.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, tr.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
			if lookFlat.Magnitude < 0.05 then
				lookFlat = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
			else
				lookFlat = lookFlat.Unit
			end
			local rightFlat = Vector3.new(-lookFlat.Z, 0, lookFlat.X)
			-- mostly BEHIND + slight side sway, ABOVE head
			local head = target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
			local headY = (head and head.Position.Y or (tr.Position.Y + 1.5))
			local offset = (-lookFlat * 2.0) + (rightFlat * sway)
			local pos = Vector3.new(
				tr.Position.X + offset.X,
				headY + 1.6,
				tr.Position.Z + offset.Z
			)

			hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(pos, aim)
			local tv = tr.AssemblyLinearVelocity
			hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(tv.X * 0.35, math.clamp(tv.Y, -12, 22), tv.Z * 0.35)
			hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

			if hrp.Position.Y < tr.Position.Y - 1.5 then
				hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(pos.X, headY + 1.6, pos.Z), aim)
			end

			if (hrp.Position - tr.Position).Magnitude <= 14 then
				tryHitTP()
			end
		end)
	end

	local function stopTPBat()
		if tpBatConn then
			tpBatConn:Disconnect()
			tpBatConn = nil
		end
		TP_BAT_ACTIVE = false
		_tpWasActive = false
		pcall(function()
			local char = LP.Character
			local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if hum then
				hum.AutoRotate = true
				hum.PlatformStand = false
			end
			if hrp and sethiddenproperty then
				sethiddenproperty(hrp, "PhysicsRepRootPart", hrp)
			end
		end)
	end

	-- re-enable TP BAT after character respawn if it was on
	pcall(function()
		if not _G._tpBatCharHook then
			_G._tpBatCharHook = true
			LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
				task.wait(0.35)
				if _tpWasActive and TP_BAT_ACTIVE then
					if tpBatConn then
						pcall(function() tpBatConn:Disconnect() end)
						tpBatConn = nil
					end
					startTPBat()
					if setMBTPBat then pcall(function() setMBTPBat(true) end) end
				end
			end)
		end
	end)



	-- _mbBtnRefs declared at main chunk level
	-- _mbBtnSetters declared at main chunk level

		local MB_C_IDLE = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 12)
		local MB_C_HOV = Color3.fromRGB(60, 4, 4)
		local MB_C_ON = Color3.fromRGB(230, 18, 18)
		local MB_C_TEXT = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
		local MB_C_TEXT_ON = Color3.fromRGB(5, 5, 5)
		local MB_C_STROKE = Color3.fromRGB(240, 24, 24)
		local MB_C_STROKE_OFF = Color3.fromRGB(70, 10, 10)

	-- Button positions matching screenshot (10 circular buttons)
	_mbBtnPositions = { -- updated by applyMobileBtnScale
		-- [1] TP BAT
		UDim2.new(1, -(MB_BTN_W*3 + MB_PAD*2 + 16), 0.12, 0),
		-- [2] AUTO LEFT
		UDim2.new(1, -(MB_BTN_W*2 + MB_PAD + 16), 0.12, 0),
		-- [3] AUTO RIGHT
		UDim2.new(1, -(MB_BTN_W + 16), 0.12, 0),
		-- [4] ANTI TP DOWN
		UDim2.new(1, -(MB_BTN_W*3 + MB_PAD*2 + 16), 0.12, MB_BTN_H + MB_PAD),
		-- [5] TP DOWN
		UDim2.new(1, -(MB_BTN_W*2 + MB_PAD + 16), 0.12, MB_BTN_H + MB_PAD),
		-- [6] LAGGER
		UDim2.new(1, -(MB_BTN_W + 16), 0.12, MB_BTN_H + MB_PAD),
		-- [7] AUTO BAT
		UDim2.new(1, -(MB_BTN_W*2 + MB_PAD + 16), 0.12, (MB_BTN_H + MB_PAD)*2),
		-- [8] CARRY SPD
		UDim2.new(1, -(MB_BTN_W + 16), 0.12, (MB_BTN_H + MB_PAD)*2),
		-- [9] DROP BR
		UDim2.new(1, -(MB_BTN_W*2 + MB_PAD + 16), 0.12, (MB_BTN_H + MB_PAD)*3),
		-- [10] BYPASS BAT
		UDim2.new(1, -(MB_BTN_W + 16), 0.12, (MB_BTN_H + MB_PAD)*3),
	}

		local function applyMobileBtnShape()
			local isRect = State.mobileBtnSquare
			local isBox = State.mobileBtnBox
			local radius = (isRect or isBox) and UDim.new(0, 8) or UDim.new(1, 0)
			local targetW = isRect and 92 or MB_BTN_W
			local targetH = isRect and 48 or MB_BTN_H
			for _, btn in ipairs(_mbBtnRefs) do
				if btn then
					btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, targetW, 0, targetH)
					for _, child in ipairs(btn:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("UICorner") then
						child.CornerRadius = radius
					end
				end
				local innerBtn = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextButton")
				if innerBtn then
					for _, child in ipairs(innerBtn:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("UICorner") then
							child.CornerRadius = radius
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function makeMobileBtn(labelText, idx, onClick)
		local container = Instance.new("Frame", gui)
		container.Name = "MB_" .. labelText:gsub("\n", "_")
		container.Size = UDim2.new(0, MB_BTN_W, 0, MB_BTN_H)
		container.Position = _mbBtnPositions[idx] or UDim2.new(1, -(MB_BTN_W + 16), 0.12, 0)
		container.BackgroundColor3 = MB_C_IDLE
		container.BorderSizePixel = 0
		container.Active = true
		container.ZIndex = 50
		Instance.new("UICorner", container).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

		local contStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", container)
		contStroke.Color = MB_C_STROKE_OFF
		contStroke.Thickness = 1.5
		contStroke.Transparency = 0.3

		local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", container)
		btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		btn.BackgroundColor3 = MB_C_IDLE
		btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
		btn.Text = ""
		btn.AutoButtonColor = false
		btn.ZIndex = 52
		Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

		local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", btn)
		lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		lbl.TextColor3 = MB_C_TEXT
		lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		lbl.TextSize = 11
		lbl.TextWrapped = true
		lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
		lbl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		lbl.ZIndex = 56
		lbl.Text = labelText

		local BG_IDLE = MB_C_IDLE

		btn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
			TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.05), {BackgroundColor3 = MB_C_HOV}):Play()
		end)
		btn.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
			TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.10), {BackgroundColor3 = BG_IDLE}):Play()
		end)
		btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
			TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {BackgroundColor3 = MB_C_HOV}):Play()
			TweenService:Create(contStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {Color = MB_C_STROKE, Transparency = 0}):Play()
		end)
		btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
			TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {BackgroundColor3 = BG_IDLE}):Play()
			TweenService:Create(contStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {Color = MB_C_STROKE_OFF, Transparency = 0.3}):Play()
		end)

		-- click works every press (tap vs drag)
		local _mbClickLock = 0
		local _pressPos = nil
		local _didDrag = false
		local DRAG_THRESHOLD = 14

		local function fireClick()
			local now = tick()
			if now - _mbClickLock < 0.08 then return end
			_mbClickLock = now
			if onClick then pcall(onClick) end
		end

		btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			if _didDrag then return end
			fireClick()
		end)
		btn.Activated:Connect(function()
			if _didDrag then return end
			fireClick()
		end)

		btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
			if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				_pressPos = inp.Position
				_didDrag = false
			end
		end)
		btn.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
			if not _pressPos then return end
			if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				local d = (inp.Position - _pressPos).Magnitude
				if d > DRAG_THRESHOLD then _didDrag = true end
			end
		end)
		btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
			if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
			-- tap = click (even if MouseButton1Click missed because of drag handler)
			if _pressPos and not _didDrag then
				fireClick()
			end
			_pressPos = nil
		end)

		local function setMBState(on)
			if on then
				TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundColor3 = MB_C_ON}):Play()
				TweenService:Create(lbl, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {TextColor3 = MB_C_TEXT_ON}):Play()
				TweenService:Create(contStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {Color = MB_C_STROKE, Transparency = 0}):Play()
			else
				TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundColor3 = BG_IDLE}):Play()
				TweenService:Create(lbl, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {TextColor3 = MB_C_TEXT}):Play()
				TweenService:Create(contStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {Color = MB_C_STROKE_OFF, Transparency = 0.3}):Play()
			end
		end

		_mbBtnRefs[idx] = container
		_mbBtnSetters[idx] = setMBState
		task.spawn(function() while container and container.Parent do TweenService:Create(contStroke,TweenInfo.new(0.85,Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),{Color=Color3.fromRGB(255,38,38),Thickness=2.2}):Play(); task.wait(0.85); TweenService:Create(contStroke,TweenInfo.new(0.85,Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),{Color=MB_C_STROKE_OFF,Thickness=1.2}):Play(); task.wait(0.85) end end)
		-- drag only when moved past threshold (handled above + makeDraggable)
		makeDraggable(container, btn)
		return setMBState
	end

		local setMBAutoBat, setMBAimV2
		-- forward decls for mutual exclusive toggles
		-- BUTTON 1: TP BAT (Far Left)
		local setMBTPBat = makeMobileBtn("TP\nBAT", 1, function()
			if TP_BAT_ACTIVE then
				stopTPBat()
			else
				startTPBat()
			end
		end)

		-- BUTTON 2: AUTO LEFT (Far Left - Top)
		local setMBAutoLeft = makeMobileBtn("AUTO\nLEFT", 2, function()
		if State.autoPlayEnabled and State.autoPlaySide == "left" then
			State.autoPlayEnabled = false
			stopAutoPlay()
		else
			State.autoPlaySide = "left"
			if State.autoPlayEnabled then stopAutoPlay() end
			State.autoPlayEnabled = true
			startAutoPlay()
		end
		if setAutoPlayUI then setAutoPlayUI(State.autoPlayEnabled) end
		autoSaveConfig()
	end)

		-- BUTTON 3: AUTO RIGHT (Far Right - Top)
		local setMBAutoRight = makeMobileBtn("AUTO\nRIGHT", 3, function()
		if State.autoPlayEnabled and State.autoPlaySide == "right" then
			State.autoPlayEnabled = false
			stopAutoPlay()
		else
			State.autoPlaySide = "right"
			if State.autoPlayEnabled then stopAutoPlay() end
			State.autoPlayEnabled = true
			startAutoPlay()
		end
		if setAutoPlayUI then setAutoPlayUI(State.autoPlayEnabled) end

	end)

	-- Store references for hide/show
	local _mbVisible = true
	local function showMobileButtons() 
		_mbVisible = true
		for _, btn in ipairs(_mbBtnRefs) do if btn then btn.Visible = true end end
	end
	local function hideMobileButtons() 
		_mbVisible = false
		for _, btn in ipairs(_mbBtnRefs) do if btn then btn.Visible = false end end
	end

	-- Sync with GUI hide/show

	local _origHideGui = hideGui
	showGui = function() _origShowGui(); showMobileButtons() end
	hideGui = function() _origHideGui(); hideMobileButtons() end

	-- Scale mobile buttons with menu scale
	local function scaleMobileButtons(scale)
			local newW = math.floor((State.mobileBtnSquare and 92 or MB_BTN_W) * scale)
			local newH = math.floor((State.mobileBtnSquare and 48 or MB_BTN_H) * scale)
			for _, btn in ipairs(_mbBtnRefs) do
				if btn then
					btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, newW, 0, newH)
			end
		end
	end

				if _autoPlayRunning then return end
				_autoPlayRunning = true
				pcall(function()
					if type(_G.SLAXER_StopAutoPlaySCR) == "function" then _G.SLAXER_StopAutoPlaySCR() end
				end)
				local src = _G.SLAXER_AUTOPLAY_SRC
				if type(src) == "string" then
					local fn, err = loadstring(src)
					if fn then task.spawn(fn) else warn("[SLAXER] AUTO PLAY:", err); _autoPlayRunning = false end
				else
					warn("[SLAXER] AUTO PLAY source missing"); _autoPlayRunning = false
				end
			else
				_autoPlayRunning = false
				pcall(function()
					if type(_G.SLAXER_StopAutoPlaySCR) == "function" then _G.SLAXER_StopAutoPlaySCR() end
				end)
			end
			pcall(autoSaveConfig)
		end)

	local _laggerV2Running = false
